# Voynich active state — structural reset baseline

Updated: 2026-08-12. Read this file first, then
`experiments/semantic_assumptions/ACTIVE_EXPERIMENT_LEDGER.tsv`.

## Outcome

- The manuscript is **not translated**. There are zero confirmed English
  lexemes, zero plaintext clauses, and no established language, phonetic
  alphabet, cipher, part-of-speech system, or S/V/O order.
- Every attempted transferable meaning assignment failed, stopped for lack of
  authorial support, was invalidated, or remained source-only and unscored.
- OCR and automated/neural image-recognition outputs are method-invalid by
  explicit user policy. Every conclusion that materially depends on them,
  positive or negative, has zero active evidential weight.
- The accumulated semantic investigation is represented by the compact active
  ledger and closed-route registry. Only its retained primary-evidence subset
  remains under `archive_pre_reset_2026-08-06/`; do not load it by default.
- The active investigation has no word guess or source-family identification.
  A source-only audit now gives the wider contemporary **astrological-medical
  compendium** class the strongest document prior. The strict Schürstab
  Iatromathematical Housebook recension is not a good whole-manuscript fit and
  is not identified as a source. Medical-alchemical preparation, illustrated
  herbal/health, and practical-entry layers remain compatible modules. This is
  a document-practice prior, not a language, text, or lexical result.
- Three current public claims have been source-audited and supply no admissible
  translation anchor. The Honeycutt operator-system deposit lacks the public
  form-to-class data and numeric reconstruction claimed on its website and
  depends on excluded AI/visual inputs. Voynichdoc explicitly publishes
  modernized narratives rather than source-faithful translations and defers
  its keys, alignment, and method. The latest DANI/Syriac deposit does publish
  a coverage script and lexicon, but omits its exact corpus and the code/scores
  for its permutation, language-comparison, and plant claims; its metadata and
  PDF disagree on the null result, its test is not held out, and it admits no
  connected specialist-read Syriac prose. None of their words, dates,
  languages, system roles, or plaintext enters the active model. These audits
  hold the public evidence out; they do not prove the underlying unpublished
  hypotheses false.
- The best active description is a manuscript-wide hierarchical, page-
  conditioned, line-reset construction system. “Record-like” is an
  organizational analogy at manuscript scale. The former EAS001 graphical-slot
  bridge is no longer active confirmed evidence: its exact positive ledger
  summary survives, but its inventory, preregistration, scorer, result, and
  validator do not survive in the repository and cannot be reconstructed.

## Confirmed structural baseline

1. Source separators carry real hierarchical information, although they need
   not be European-style word boundaries. They must be kept in their four
   manual IVTFF states: confident apparent space, uncertain small space,
   drawing interruption, and unaligned drawing interruption. The legacy ASCII
   cleaner also created 2,714 additional fragment boundaries that are not
   source separators and must never be treated as human-marked spaces.
   A no-alignment consensus scaffold retains 3,572 shared loci with no
   bracketed alternatives and exactly the same complete STA-family sequence in
   all three readings. Of 20,523 union boundary positions there, 19,709
   (96.03%) occur in at least two readings and 19,041 occur in all three. These
   are source-synchronized transcription boundaries, not proven authorial
   words.
2. Multi-unit forms are productively compositional; the inventory is not well
   described as unrelated opaque codebook items.
3. Physical lines behave as record/utterance-like units. A validated writing-
   order/content coordinate rises within a line and resets at the next line.
4. The frozen retained parser records directional formal-state edge classes,
   but their strongest surface carrier is not novel syntax. After source-group
   correction, 4,731/4,737 registered hard edges directly join adjacent source
   groups; 4,247/4,731 (89.77%) are literal `y|q`, 4,564/4,731 (96.47%)
   enter `qo...`, and 4,095/4,731 (86.56%) are `dy|qo` or `ey|qo`. This
   strongly overlaps the publicly documented `y.q`/`qo` word-boundary pattern.
   Preserve the exact source-separator evidence and the six conditional role
   partitions, but do not cite them as an independently established new
   syntax: the role labels depend on the unavailable, surface-incomplete
   parser. The direct boundaries remain 4,699 confident apparent spaces, 26
   uncertain small spaces, and six drawing interruptions; six further edges
   skip a source group and are excluded. Detached REL_I, FREE_L, and FREE_R
   completions also occur across source separators, still without meaning.
5. Bare d/s/t line-entry carriers form a qualified state system. Bare t is
   associated with ZL-editor-marked paragraph openings; bare d/s are associated
   with marked continuations. The association is real, but the boundaries are
   editorial reconstructions of layout rather than authorial semantic tags.
   These are not START or CONTINUE words.
6. Strict `AII+N` tends to precede strict `AI+N` within a physical line,
   especially in Currier B. This is not counting or a number system.
7. Roots show a weak but held left-to-right rise toward content/identifier-like
   usage. It is distributed rather than a fixed sentence field.
8. Exact `che+VALUE` is a highly productive carrier construction. Its relative
   low-content-to-high-content direction is qualified across source ecologies;
   neither `che` nor its values have English meanings.
9. Literal-root inventories have qualified page-scale coherence. In a frozen
   held test, nonadjacent same-page lines exceeded form/length-matched
   cross-page pairs (ZL weighted-Jaccard +0.00974, +13.4%, Holm p=0.00718;
   IT/RF same direction). Immediately adjacent lines did not exceed matched
   nonadjacent same-page lines. A later sealed-root conditional test found no
   material physical-line recency or paragraph-only clustering after exact
   form/position controls (both ZL residuals about +0.0021; Holm p=0.492).
   At this resolution the effect is an order-free page inventory, not proof of
   page topic or semantics.
10. Adjacent literal-root identities have held selection structure beyond that
    page inventory. With page vocabulary, exact form shells, horizontal
    position, entry state, stratum, and every D/C edge location fixed, ZL real
    adjacency exceeds conditional permutations by +0.03518 bit/edge (69.4%
    positive pages, conservative p=0.000488); IT/RF share the direction. The
    scoring table was learned on other page buckets, so this is transferable
    cross-page assembly rather than a separate fit within each scored page.
    By itself it did not establish transfer across Currier/section/hand
    strata; the follow-up in item 11 does. Neither result establishes a POS,
    phrase label, or meaning.
    A powered follow-up found no held broad attraction or repulsion in edit,
    prefix, suffix, bigram, or length shape, so simple adjacent transcription-
    shape copying does not explain this identity structure at that resolution.
11. The aggregate adjacency relation transfers across major metadata
    boundaries. When all training pages sharing the target Currier value,
    section, or catalogued hand are excluded, held ZL residuals remain
    +0.03023, +0.02819, and +0.03391 bit/edge (joint-family p=0.00196,
    0.00244, and 0.000488); IT/RF keep the same positive directions with
    sufficient page reuse. This supports one shared manuscript-wide
    construction system. Because Currier, section, and hand are correlated,
    the axes are not independent effects and do not establish authorship.
12. The shared Currier A/B relation also contains direction-specific partner
    information beyond unordered D-C pair affinity and root-side propensity.
    With the target Currier value excluded from training, held ZL gains
    +0.01725 bit/edge on 80.6% of 36/44 pages (joint-family p=0.0171); IT/RF
    remain positive with 100%/94.4% page reuse. Section and hand directional
    increments did not confirm. This is a physical-order constraint, not a
    subject/verb/object analysis or proof of spoken reading direction.
13. The fixed Timm local self-citation process does not reproduce that
    cross-Currier directional relation. Under an exact expectation of IL006's
    conditional null, ZL scores +0.01692 bit/edge on 80.6% of 36/44 pages.
    Among 64 sealed generated manuscripts, 62 passed frozen eligibility; none
    matched either the real residual or its positive-page fraction
    (conservative plus-one p=0.01587). This rejects that fixed generator as a
    sufficient account of item 12, not synthetic generation generally, and it
    does not establish ordinary language or meaning.
14. The cross-Currier directional relation is not confirmed to require a
    transferable non-additive cycle component beyond root-side directional
    propensities. A weighted Hodge projection removed the best additive D-root
    plus C-root potential before scoring. The powered held ZL increment was
    +0.00457 bit/edge on 72.2% of 36/44 pages, but missed both the frozen
    +0.005 magnitude gate and p<=.05 gate (p=.130649); IT/RF were positive but
    weaker. This does not identify a system class.
15. The complementary additive scalar/root-propensity account is also not
    confirmed as a material explanation. Every Currier-A page was predicted
    only from Currier-B pages and conversely. ZL was consistently positive at
    +0.00272 bit/edge on 55.4% of 157/197 pages (raw p=.00830; sequentially
    adjusted p=.01659), and IT/RF remained positive, but the effect missed the
    frozen +0.005 materiality gate. Thus IL010's full directional relation
    remains real but is not explained at useful magnitude by either compact
    scalar or isolated cycle components at these frozen resolutions.

The historical EAS001 ledger reports 46 arrays, 391 slots, 374 transitions,
13 folios, minimum effect +.2942, and p=.005859. Those numbers are retained as
routing history, not as reconstructed evidence. All nine named scientific
paths are absent, as are all five hash-recorded precursor bodies; no reachable
or dangling Git object recovers them. The exact folio list is unavailable, so
the newer public correction that f67--f73 are seven folios on four bifolios
cannot be applied to its synchronous null. EAS001 and all claims depending on
its frozen inventory are therefore `PROVISIONAL_HISTORICAL_UNRECONSTRUCTED`
until hash-matching artifacts are recovered or a new versioned experiment is
run. This provenance hold does not show that the historical effect was false.

A new versioned inventory now satisfies only the second half of that reopen
condition, without reconstructing the old panel or inheriting its score. A
filler-blind mechanical scan of the current human exact-locus annotation table
selects 45 arrays and 504 slots, including 502 transcribed label/radial
positions, one unreadable-trace position at f67v2.21, and one explicitly absent
label at f72r2.33, across 23 panels on all seven
physical folios f67--f73. Selection uses only page, unit, and human layout
code; no surface, family, member, root, parser role, or image feature enters.
Its 45/504/7 scope is explicitly different from historical EAS001's 46/391/13
scope. The new data layer authorizes only a separate score-blind design and
does not establish record boundaries or cross-diagram slot equivalence.
The two nontranscribed rows are not homologous omissions: f67v2.21 retains
unreadable ink traces, whereas f72r2.33 is explicitly unlabelled. With only
one secure absent slot on one folio, no omission-pattern test is authorized.

ZL3b, IT2a, and RF1b are alternate readings of one manuscript, never three
independent samples. Structural tags must remain distinct from translations.

## Hard evidence exclusion — OCR and automated vision

- Do not run or use OCR on Voynich text, historical sources, labels, marginalia,
  or diagrams. Existing manual ZL/IT/RF transcriptions are permitted.
- Do not run or use batch automated image recognition, detection, segmentation,
  captioning, feature embeddings, similarity search, CLIP/prompt scoring, or
  model-generated object/plant labels. The AI-generated plant candidate list
  remains excluded as evidence. Source-bound direct native visual inspection
  is permitted under the provenance and uncertainty rules below.
- Any earlier experiment that consumed an excluded output is methodologically
  flawed. Its dependent positive and negative results cannot support, close, or
  reopen a route. A mixed report is unusable until a provenance audit isolates
  a reproducible branch based entirely on permitted inputs.
- Permitted evidence is text-internal structure from manual transcriptions and
  provenance-traceable human-authored labels, descriptions, catalog data, or
  direct human inspection. Human labels must not have been generated or filled
  in by an image/OCR model.

On 2026-08-11 the user first authorized a narrow f57v capability test and then
authorized direct native AI visual inspection generally. Every inspected
image must be an exact source-bound official or provenance-traceable witness;
observations must distinguish visible geometry from interpretation, record
uncertainty and corrections, and never be represented as literal human
annotation. OCR, CLIP, embeddings, similarity scoring, unconstrained object or
plant naming, and automatic semantic transfer remain excluded, and the policy
change does not retroactively admit any earlier excluded output.

The first source-bound pilot finds four annular writing bands and four
inward-facing central figures around a rosette, with four figure-near
inscriptions and four radial inscriptions between the figures. No drawn
leader, bracket, or separate enclosure assigns a nearby inscription to a
specific figure, so the existing `PROXIMITY_ONLY` ownership state remains.
Comparison with the opposite side of the same leaf, f57r, shows that f57v's
pale green wash corresponds to mirrored green-painted plant masses on f57r.
Classify this as reverse-side show-through or pigment penetration; the scans
alone do not distinguish optical translucency from physical pigment migration.
Neither observation supplies a lexical value or translation.

## What is deliberately inactive

Botanical identification, literal bathing labels, zodiac/astronomical
dictionaries, number systems, image proximity, direct historical source-text
matching, known-language alignment, cipher/codebook work, and all named word
guesses are archive-only. Comparative historical manuscript practice is
permitted only to constrain document assumptions; it is not a lexical anchor.
OCR, CLIP, embedding, batch recognition, and prompt-scored vision routes remain
prohibited methods rather than dormant hypotheses. Source-bound native visual
inspection is permitted prospectively. Consult `CLOSED_ROUTE_FAMILIES.tsv`
before proposing a related test.

## Fresh research question

Before another meaning or source passage is tested, determine the actual
document roles of Voynich text units without using string similarity: heading,
entry, prose block, list item, caption, diagram parameter, or uncertain. A
direct human or source-bound native-visual codicological/layout inventory must
keep proximity separate from ownership and record explicit connectors,
enclosures, reserved writing space, text/image ordering, repeated templates,
and uncertainty. The historical
assumption gate is in
`results/historical_alignment_assumption_audit.md`. No image-to-grammar score
is authorized until this role inventory exists.

The premise that a new manual inventory had to be created before proceeding
was corrected after an exhaustive local and live-source audit. Current IVTFF
metadata already supplies editorial P/L/C/R roles for all 5,385 loci on 227
pages: 4,130 paragraph, 1,029 label, 84 circular, and 142 radial loci. ZL3b and
RF1b agree on the exact code at all 5,385 loci; IT2a agrees at all 5,214 shared
loci but is less complete. These are human editorial layout roles, not
authorial semantics or object ownership.

The active source-only atlas also retains 228 voynich.nu page records, with
illustration and named text-layout prose on 226 and literal IDs for 226/227
active pages. The catalogue-level `fRos` record remains a compound-foldout
case rather than a literal one-page match. The earlier local importer had
mistakenly omitted the catalogue's entire named `Text` field.

The 18 public quire HTML sources behind that atlas are now restored after
curation had removed them while retaining only derived artifacts. A live
2026-08-10 refresh matches every previously registered source SHA-256 and
independently reproduces all 228 page IDs plus every stored general,
illustration, text, tentative-identification, and other-information field with
zero differences. The builder now uses the active public snapshot and fails
closed if any quire is absent. This repairs provenance but contributes no new
description or semantic anchor.

A subsequent folio-boundary audit corrects the interpretation of that exact
reproduction. The cached HTML was stable, but the old parser did not clear its
active page at folio headers, so **84** verso/page records inherited the next
folio's prose in `other_information`. A versioned 228-row v2 table removes only
those suffixes; general, illustration, text, tentative-identification, source
tag, URL, and role-evidence fields are unchanged. No semantic consumer reads
the affected field, so prior scores do not change. The same audit exposes one
public-source contradiction: f73v's illustration and December label identify
Sagittarius, while its tentative-identification sentence says Scorpius.
Tentative identifications were already non-role evidence, and the independent
label catalogue calls f73v Sagittarius. Future public-page work must use v2
and must not treat tentative identities as authoritative. The public f67--f73
circle scope remains 26 page panels.

One small production-layer lead is now retained without a lexical gloss.
The human catalogue has 13 `COL`-classed records on ten pages: twelve are
plain-alphabet records, including Latin `g` under green paint on f1v, German
`rot` on f4r, and probable `rot` under paint on f7r. The sole Voynich-script
record is the green-leaf-boundary candidate `ios an on` at f2r.15. ZL3b and
RF1b agree on that surface, but IT2a omits it and `ios`/`on` are locus-unique.
A source-bound native-visual reinspection corrects the older
`DIRECT_ENCLOSURE_UNDER_PAINT` classification: the record straddles a narrow
leaf tip, with part overlapping pale green and the remainder continuing onto
bare parchment. Ordinary-light pixels do not establish whether ink preceded
or followed the wash. Retain only `BOUNDARY_OVERLAP_LAYER_ORDER_UNRESOLVED`
and a local leaf association. There is no second Voynich-script colour
contrast. No token can be glossed GREEN, pigment, or action. The plain notes
do not establish that the main-text scribe wrote them or that the main text is
German. A retained-node formal audit further narrows the record:
the parsed `a -> o` tail behind `an on` recurs at six physical loci, including
two all-reading prose loci, while the reverse order also occurs. The `a` and
`o` roots occur at 408 and 874 physical loci. Only the composite `i+os` is
locus-unique. A two-reading `e+os | oii | o` parallel in the f67r2 moon-sector
text provides a second similar short-record frame but is not all-reading
evidence.
Thus only the first slot remains record-specific, and its uniqueness makes it
untestable rather than readable. It is also not atomic: component `i` occurs
in 35 loci / 14 complete forms, including 13 `i+...` composites, while `os`
occurs in 406 loci / 104 complete forms and 70 `...+os` composites span 293
loci. The small `i` family is reading-sensitive, but `os` productivity is
supported in all three readings at 205 composite loci. An independent
34-check implementation reconstructs the component counts, productivity,
pair directions, shell, two-reading parallel, and lexical stop exactly.

Two older detailed layers are useful but incomplete. Stolfi 25e1 comments give
a nonexhaustive regex index of 1,099 object-bearing and 93 label-only loci on
78 pages; absence means unknown. Among 400 unhedged exact-local relation rows,
319 contain proximity language and 110 contain at least one stronger relation
tag, with overlapping tag counts. The 1998 Stolfi best label/title file has
1,018 described legacy records on 79 source page IDs, including 43 proposed
titles. It is not the current 1,029-L-locus inventory and the totals must not be
subtracted or treated as one-to-one coverage. Its legacy IDs `f101v1` and
`f101v2` now map explicitly to current `f101v`, and `f86r4` to current `fRos`;
this does not erase the separate catalogue-level compound-foldout caveat.

A corrected current-locus crosswalk establishes that the 1,018 source records
represent only 998 physical locations. Exactly 20 `f75v.L1` locations carry
U- and V-coded alternate human transcriptions; the superseded record-level
assignment had incorrectly forced those 40 records onto 40 different current
loci. The physical-location-first crosswalk keeps both transcriptions attached
to one locus and retains genuine duplicate strings at distinct locations. The
source format explicitly defines field 6 as the transcriber code and field 7 as
the EVA text; the importer column formerly mislabeled `best_reading` is now
`transcriber_code`, while matching continues to use the correct field-7 text.
The crosswalk conservatively maps 816/998 physical locations: 274 by explicit
human ring/Grove position, 180 by selected whole-group sequence position, and
362 by unambiguous or margin-separated page-local transcription agreement.
Twelve repeated-string locations remain explicitly `EXACT_REPEAT_AMBIGUOUS`;
none was collapsed or dropped. Across the full source, 90 exact-normalized text
types recur at 239 distinct physical locations; every occurrence remains
available for repetition analysis. There are zero conflicts between the strong
position and sequence evidence layers.

The clustered builder is reproducible and an independent validator passes all
record, physical-location, duplicate, sequence, one-to-one, matrix, alias, and
hash checks. A small post-hoc visual inspection of official Yale canvases found
no gross layout/cardinality contradiction and confirmed the `f75v` witness-unit
correction. Pixels were used only for quality control and never selected,
scored, moved, rejected, read, or glossed a mapping.

The visual witness is frozen to the single current official Yale manifest
`https://collections.library.yale.edu/manifests/2002046`, SHA-256
`317d58fd9ea90392a83d9858a91eada3d0b41416a3c835857dc0154bd123a309`;
a live refetch matched the cached hash. The older Voynichese.com/2014 scan
frame, old page cache, all word rectangles/registrations, crops, pixels, OCR,
and automated vision are excluded. Multiple current-manifest canvases for a
foldout are views of one record, never replications. Independent validation is

The optional new-annotation instrument remains frozen at
`DOCUMENT_ROLE_ANNOTATION_V2`, but it is not the next action because the user
has ruled out a new manual pass. Its 47 calibration pages, 16 diagnostic
holdouts, and synthetic scorer remain software artifacts only; existing public
annotations cannot be imported into them and then called unseen confirmation.

A design-power correction limits the 16-page holdout to a diagnostic role.
Those pages represent 16 repeated layout-template/editorial-section cells and
omit sections A and C; adding more pages inside the same cells would not add
independent cells. The holdout can test exact-cell role transfer and schema
drift, but cannot confirm a manuscript-wide compendium or other global class.

The source gathering and corrected crosswalk are complete and independently
validate as `PASS_EXISTING_HUMAN_ANNOTATION_ATLAS_VALIDATION` and
`PASS_INDEPENDENT_CLUSTERED_CURRENT_LOCUS_CROSSWALK_VALIDATION`. The next route
audited repeated exact texts against source assertions and position-only
current roles. Of 90 repeated consensus types at 239 physical locations, 81
cross pages. After excluding same-page pairs and permuting roles within pages,
source object class was only +0.01205 above null (Holm p=.5969), source object
guess +0.01710 (Holm p=.5969), and attribute tagsets -0.06105 (Holm p=.9579);
none approached the frozen +0.10 materiality gate. The position-only mapping
tier had 273 mapped loci but only one editorial kind/subtype, and no exact
unhedged relation-family overlap, so those outcomes were nondiscriminating
rather than positive. Independent reconstruction validates every duplicate,
type, permutation score, gate, and artifact.

This nonconfirmation rules out treating exact repetition by itself as a stable
coarse object-class, object-guess, attribute, or position-only editorial-role
key in the legacy public layer. It does not make repetition useless, prove
different meanings, or test finer authorial relations absent from the
annotations.

The genuinely different direct-current-locus layer then passed score-blind
coverage with 51 conservative all-reading exact types recurring across pages at
136 loci; current kind, kind/subtype, context class, and object-tag set were
frozen, while relation family remained unscored after failing coverage at 10
types/24 loci. The held direct-locus test is also a formal nonconfirmation.
Broad editorial kind showed a real but sub-material tendency: observed
type-weighted agreement .80439 versus null .71068, effect +.09371, Holm
p=.000854. It missed the predeclared +.10 materiality gate and cannot be rescued
by lowering it. Kind/subtype was only +.02720 (Holm p=.3549), context class
-.01332, and object-tag set -.02213. Two deterministic builds and an independent
32,767-permutation reconstruction reproduce every outcome and gate.

Thus exact repetition may weakly prefer the same broad editorial text kind, but
it is not confirmed as a materially stable document-role key and gives no
object or lexical meaning. No duplicate-role threshold, type subset, consensus
rule, section, outcome, or null may now be retuned. A next route must use a
genuinely different relation invariant or new data; the underpowered direct
relation-family branch cannot be scored. A public source layer is not a held
lexical annotation, and no lexical meaning follows from these results.

A source-bound native-visual worth screen now also stops the most plausible
new repeated-caption exception before text access. The human exact-locus layer
contains 14 unhedged apparatus-label rows with an explicit-attachment tag on
seven pages. Fixed comment families give five tube/pipe/channel rows, three
tub/container rows, three pool/pond rows, and four stream/outflow rows, but
most are between or near multiple objects or mix component kinds. Even under
a deliberately generous singular grade, the largest repeated component class
reaches only two physical folios. This misses the fixed three-folio worth gate;
no label string or formal feature association was opened. Do not replace
singular ownership with proximity or run an apparatus component-name decoder.

The user has now corrected the search unit from complete space-delimited texts
to recurring patterns at several levels. An archive audit found that the failed
449-row/32-page manual-label decoder already used whitespace-stripped
character, parsed-root, role, and atom n-grams, so that experiment must not be
repeated on the same panel. The new direct-current-locus layer is materially
broader: 863/1,192 loci and 47/78 pages lie outside the old analysis panel. An
initial score-blind audit found 104 eligible whitespace-agnostic root patterns,
but the user then clarified that spaces and complete tokens must remain useful
evidence rather than being discarded. That FLAT inventory remains a valid
subset but is superseded as the next test.

The corrected multiscale audit simultaneously retains exact tokens, proper
within-token patterns, prefixes, suffixes, infixes, cross-space sequences, and
a boundary-agnostic view, in both literal manual-transcription characters and
grammar-parsed roots. Every pattern must occur in ZL3b/RF1b and in IT2a when
present. Exact tokens must recur inside at least three different full locus
surfaces and contexts; proper pieces and cross-space sequences require at least
three different containing tokens or token pairs. This yields 2,529 supported
patterns but only 2,002 distinct physical-locus membership groups, so aliases
with identical evidence will be scored once. Independent validation checked
171,258 reading-specific occurrences and every equivalence group.

The frozen family-corrected direct-locus computation remains arithmetically
reproducible, but its circular interpretation is now retracted. It tested
binary pattern presence anywhere in a locus without controlling text exposure.
In that scored panel the 24 `C:c` loci average 28.62 ZL tokens, versus 5.70 for
paragraph, 1.91 for radial, and 1.15 for label loci. Thus the reported 52/54
fine-role hits selecting circular text—including literal cross-space `l|o`,
`y|o`, exact `aiin`, root `aii`, and root `ol`—had far more chances to occur in
a circular locus. The old corrected p values do not establish a circular
register marker independent of length. None of the 2,002 groups passed the old
context/object outcomes, but those branches also retain their separately
documented annotation-design limits.

A later cyclic follow-up exposed an essential parser distinction that must be
kept active. Exact surface `aiin` is **not** a strict `AII+N` minim-grade form:
it parses as root `aii`, stage2 `NONE`, final `N`, and the active grammar renders
it as root `aii` with role `BARE`. Across the circular inventory, zero of 44
ZL, 40 IT, or 59 RF exact-`aiin` occurrences belongs to strict `AII+N`; forms
such as `daiin`, `otaiin`, and `kaiin` instantiate that separate confirmed
grade system. Never transfer the prose AII-before-AI result to bare `aiin`.

The frozen CC001 cut test is therefore only a strict-minim diagnostic, not an
exact-`aiin` experiment. It also nonconfirms on its own terms: held ZL `LO`,
`YO`, and their union have effects +.3718, +.1338, and +.2882 but maxT
p=.1965/.6435/.2837, only 5/7/7 eligible rings, and only 5/7/7 AI events.
The independently validated rotation-invariant inventory still descriptively
covers all 84 ZL/RF circular loci and 72 IT loci. In ZL it counts 77 root-`aii`
nodes, 44 exact surface `aiin` realizations, 61 root-`ol` nodes, 130 `l|o`
edges, and 342 `y|o` edges. These are valid counts inside long rings, but they
are no longer established circular-register anchors or separators.

CC002 directly audited that exposure problem on old-panel-excluded data. Only
24 held C loci on 10 pages retained same-page held non-C comparators after all
1,192 old direct loci were excluded; 36 other held C loci were not silently
dropped but were ineligible for this page-paired design. All six frozen rate
outcomes failed. ZL circular/non-circular rate ratios are .757 for exact
`aiin`, .764 for root `aii`, .598 for root `ol`, 1.175 for cyclic `l|o`, and
1.037 for cyclic `y|o`; Holm p is 1.0 except `y|o` at .656. Conditional on an
existing root-`aii` position, exact `aiin` occurs at 44.0% in circular versus
43.5% in same-page non-circular text (ratio 1.012; only 1/4 nonzero page
contrasts positive). Production controls and independent 467-control/3,717-
result checks pass. Therefore exact `aiin` remains root `aii`/BARE, but no
circular-specific realization is established. Do not pursue an `aiin`-anchored
circular template route without genuinely new evidence. CC003 below performs
the permitted length-normalized whole-construction test rather than mining
another common token, stem, edge, or template.

CC003 is complete with a validated whole-construction nonconfirmation. It
trained one root-free joint cyclic model on the 24 previously exposed C loci,
using exact compound formal-role shells plus boundary-role transitions, and
scored all 60 held ZL rings against each ring's analytic exact-length,
exact-shell-multiset cycle expectation. The held gain is only +.001856
bit/edge across 24 pages, with 13/23 nonzero pages positive and Holm p=.6776;
it misses the +.010 magnitude, page-consistency, leave-one-out, and section
gates. Section Z is negative at -.002654; combined A/C is +.005673 but only
8/12 nonzero pages positive (p=.3877).

The independent prose-specificity outcome is also negative. After a pre-score
correction removed 95 `P` loci whose grammar scope was diagnostic non-prose,
24 section-C ZL rings on nine pages retained 32 deterministic confirmed-prose
windows each, matched exactly in word count without crossing a page. Ring gain
minus median prose-window gain is -.040399 bit/edge, with only 2/9 pages
positive and Holm p=.3594; IT/RF also fail. Controls independently pass 556
checks and the result reconstruction passes 4,137. Thus neither the earlier
specific forms nor this aggregate first-order role-shell ordering provides a
transferable circular grammar anchor. Do not retune shells, smoothing, window
matching, sections, thresholds, or individual transitions. This does not prove
that circular texts lack meaning or higher-order structure; it closes this
specific length-normalized first-order construction route.

A post-hoc support/power audit corrects the scope of that negative statement.
The frozen zero-pass result is valid, but the seven-tag omnibus is not fair
evidence that no illustration-associated pattern exists. `ANIMAL` has no
within-page mobility at all. The global null maximum is controlled by
WATER_OR_APPARATUS in 83.3% of permutations, PLANT in 13.2%, and STAR_OR_SKY in
3.5%, with a median |z| threshold of 12.53. The tags are also strongly coupled
to editorial roles; after stratifying by page and role, only 6 variable strata
remain for PLANT, 8 for STAR_OR_SKY, 12 for WATER_OR_APPARATUS, and 14 for
FIGURE. Therefore retain “zero passes under the frozen omnibus,” but replace
“no illustration-associated stem” with **inconclusive under an imbalanced,
role-confounded object-tag design**. Post-hoc tag-only p-values are diagnostics,
not rescued semantic hits; in particular they do not license `lcheo` as a
FIGURE marker or `yo` as a LABEL marker. Any future object test must freeze a
minimum-support/mobility gate and control page-by-editorial-role before seeing
scores, and must say explicitly when the surviving human annotations are too
sparse for confirmation.

That proposed page-by-role remedy was itself audited and is now superseded.
The resulting 565-cell run has zero passes, but all 32,767 pooled null maxima
are controlled by the sparse PLANT channel. More importantly, deterministic
reconstruction shows that the arithmetic is answering the wrong question for
illustration stems. The 1,192 indexed loci come from only 239 human annotation
units; object tags are regex matches over a **combined unit description plus
local comment**, so one unit description may be copied to as many as 32 loci.
Absence of a tag is UNKNOWN/not mentioned, not a certified negative, and the
extractor has no negation scope: nine rows tagged LABEL include an explicit
“no label” or “not labeled” phrase. Of all positive rows, exact page-by-role
conditioning leaves variance for only 13/261 PLANT, 16/502 STAR_OR_SKY,
45/406 FIGURE, and 31/136 WATER_OR_APPARATUS loci. It removes between-page
category evidence and then conditions on editorial subtypes such as `L:f`,
`L:s`, and `L:z` that are already almost category codes. The surviving rows
also have major text-length differences, while binary pattern presence was not
exposure-normalized.

Therefore both zero-pass object-tag experiments are **valid narrow
computations but invalid for closing the illustration-pattern/stem route**.
No illustration stem is established, but none is excluded. The earlier rule
requiring page-by-exact-role shuffling must not be reused. A corrected route
must keep page, annotation unit, and exact-local relation as distinct evidence
levels; retain UNKNOWN and explicit absence separately; use independent page
or unit clusters for page-level categories; normalize text exposure; and use
only unhedged strong local relation rows for primary locus-ownership evidence.
Proximity-only rows and sparse strong-local sets may nominate candidates but
cannot support a route-closing negative test.

The first attempted positive-only attached-label/attribute follow-up is
**invalidated before admission**. Its parser conflated attachment certainty
with unrelated glyph/attribute uncertainty, treated plural group descriptions
as locus ownership, mishandled ambiguous core descriptions, called two panels
of one folio independent pages, combined lexical and structural search
families, and retained length/exposure-biased global candidate rankings. Its
subsequent 30-check control is also invalid: it imported the implementation it
was meant to validate, used one null at 999 rather than 9,999 permutations,
and changed a null threshold after seeing p=.067. No count, candidate, p-value,
or route conclusion from those artifacts is active. Before any rerun, freeze
an adversarial gold parser set, separate reference implementation, exact small
sample enumeration and null calibration, lexical/structural families,
same-folio dependence, attribute-level ambiguity, strict JSON, input hashes,
and post-write artifact verification.

That repair loop is now complete as attached-label v2. The frozen gate was
expanded before scoring to 38 adversarial parser cases, including the real
`Dirt? Seven points` sentence-boundary case and an explicitly merged two-label
locus; exact 4,900 balanced assignments, 1,296 within-length assignments,
40,320 spatial permutations, repeated numeric-value multiplicities, strict
serialization, and source hashes all pass a non-importing validator. The human
source contains 53 f68r1/f68r2 star-label records. One f68r2 locus combines two
label records while describing only one star's attributes, so it is excluded;
the scored inventory is 52 physical loci (29 and 23). These are two panels of
one folio context, not independent confirmations.

All 17 frozen point-count, core-state, distance, sparse replicated-point, and
spatial tests completed. **No lexical-root, literal-surface, structural-role,
spatial, or length-control result passes the complete gates.** The smallest
raw p is a structural `BOUND_E` association with seven-point stars in the
sparse test (raw p=.0059), but it fails the 17-test Holm correction
(p=.1003) and the frozen effect gate (.2981 < .35); it is not a stem or
meaning. The strongest lexical distance diagnostic is `ROOT_ATOM:ot` at
|r|=.4475, but its familywise raw p=.1685 and length-conditioned p=.2217 are
null. A core-state/length diagnostic has raw p=.0332 but Holm p=.5312. A
separate non-importing result validator rebuilt all 52 loci, feature aliases,
17 statistics, every 9,999-permutation null, length sensitivities, Holm gates,
and hashes in 981 checks.

This is a valid **narrow nonconfirmation**, not a global closure of
illustration-associated morphology. It says these particular stable feature
families do not reliably encode the later annotator's star point count, core
state, recorded radius, or local geometry at the frozen resolution. Do not
retune the thresholds, promote the structural near-miss, split the merged
record by guess, or reuse the invalid v1 candidates. A rerun needs genuinely
new provenance-traceable one-to-one human attributes or an authorial anchor,
not another subset of these same 52 loci.

A preliminary `pc`/OBJECT_BEARING hit was rejected before interpretation: the
observed statistic was float64 while the GPU null was float32, differing by
less than 0.000001 at an attainable discrete maximum. Recomputing the unchanged
frozen statistic in float64 gives corrected p=.618042 and zero context passes.
Independent reconstruction repeated all 32,767 permutations for all 7,766
group/outcome scores and confirms editorial-role association only. Do not use
the invalid preliminary context result or promote circular-role patterns to
lexical meanings.

Before assigning another meaning, determine which broad system class best
predicts the confirmed text-internal grammar: ordinary language, structured
notation, mnemonic/record system, or a generative/synthetic process. No class
is currently preferred. Use only manual transcription and provenance-traceable
human-authored metadata. A future experiment must state a discriminating held
prediction; fitting another descriptive model is insufficient.

`IL002` is complete: exact-root overlap is page-associated but not specifically
adjacency-associated under its frozen contrasts. The next route must distinguish
page-level content/register from local production or orthographic persistence,
preferably by a preregistered paragraph/page/bifolio/quire scale test with
independent root partitions. IL001's accumulating cache remains invalid and
never inspected its final holdout.

`IL003` stopped at validation because exact-stratum cross-quire controls covered
only 55.2% of Q targets versus the frozen 80% requirement. It made no discovery
selection and computed no confirmation-root similarity. Its development values
are not claims and may not be used to choose a physical scale.

`IL004` is complete with a held nonconfirmation. Its fixed D-to-C association
table scored ZL at +0.00693 bit/C-root, 51.2% positive pages, and p=0.151 on
41/44 eligible held pages, below every frozen material threshold. IT/RF had the
same positive direction but are alternate readings, not replications. Thus
IL002 is not upgraded to a distributed cross-root page signature; only this
fixed association invariant is closed.

`IL005` is complete. Both frozen conditional-recurrence scopes failed held ZL
materiality: page-conditioned +0.00208 (+1.07%), 54.8% positive, Holm p=0.492;
paragraph-conditioned +0.00206 (+1.00%), 47.2% positive, Holm p=0.492. IT2a
alone showed a larger excess, while RF1b and primary ZL did not; this is
transcription sensitivity, not replication. IL002 therefore behaves as an
order-free exact-root page inventory at this resolution.

`IL006` is complete and confirmed. `IL007` passed all frozen validation gates
on 45/52 eligible pages. Of 12 training-frozen oriented pairs, only P01
(`s -> aii` in normalized physical-root order) advanced: 24 edges on 18 pages
versus 12.584 under the page-conditioned null (+11.416, +90.7%, joint max-z
p=0.000488). An archive-overlap audit then found that this same pair had
already been explicitly tested in the text-only exact lexical-transition atlas
and native composition graph. IL007 is therefore a stricter re-test, not a
novel pair discovery; it is stopped without scoring bucket 0. No phrase,
gloss, or POS label is inferred.

`IL008` is complete with a powered nonconfirmation. Its bucket-1 validation
passed all six frozen gates, but no bucket-0 feature confirmed. ZL edit
similarity came closest at +0.00497 on 63.9% of 36/44 pages, but missed the
absolute threshold, had conservative p=0.340, and RF had the opposite sign.
Thus neither broad adjacent transcription-shape attraction nor repulsion
explains IL006 at the frozen resolution. The next route must test a genuinely
different production or system-class invariant, not another string distance,
feature subset, direction, threshold, split, or pair list.

`IL009` is complete and confirmed on all three axes. Held ZL Currier-, section-,
and hand-exclusion residuals are +0.03023, +0.02819, and +0.03391 bit/edge,
with joint-family p=0.00196, 0.00244, and 0.000488. IT/RF reuse 100%/94.4% of
the ZL pages and remain positive on every axis. This upgrades the local
assembly layer to a shared manuscript-wide construction relation across the
named metadata boundaries. It does not establish ordinary language,
authorship, POS, sound, or meaning. The next route should test whether this
shared relation is low-dimensional/rule-like or an irreducibly lexical pair
network, without reopening exact-pair mining.

A generic class-compression rerun is blocked by archived substitution-class
work. `IL010` is the registered next route instead: it tests whether IL009's
shared relation contains direction-specific D-C partner information beyond an
orientation-collapsed pair table. All three metadata axes are frozen jointly
after a powered but mixed training-only audit. `CURRIER` confirmed in held ZL
at +0.01725 bit/edge, 80.6% positive pages, joint-family p=0.0171, with IT/RF
positive. `SECTION` and `HAND` did not confirm. The active model therefore has
a shared cross-Currier directional relation, but no physical-to-spoken reading
direction, phrase label, POS, language, sound, or meaning. The next route must
connect this direction to an independently confirmed structural coordinate or
test higher-order organization without reopening archived class, exact-pair,
or long-distance-binding searches.

`IL011` is complete and confirms a narrow process exclusion. The exact-null ZL
Currier-direction residual is +0.01692 bit/edge with 80.6% positive pages.
Zero of 62 eligible held Timm controls matched either statistic, giving the
frozen conservative p=0.01587. The cached fixed local self-citation generator
is therefore insufficient for IL010. Do not generalize this to all synthetic
or mnemonic processes, and do not rerun Timm seeds, thresholds, or parameter
tweaks as a new route. The next useful test should connect the surviving
direction to the independent line/content coordinate or confront a genuinely
different predeclared system mechanism.

The first such connection audit, `IL012`, stopped on training data and was
never registered. Its signed interaction with three independent label/prose
root coordinates was negative on all three axes, but even the post-observation
reverse signs were unpowered after family correction (p=0.695, 0.723, 0.188).
Validation and final pages were not scored. Do not reopen it by reversing the
sign, selecting BIO alone, or changing thresholds. A future coordinate bridge
needs a genuinely distinct invariant.

`IL013` then tested the independent five-bin line coordinate on training pages.
Early and late IL010 residuals were both near zero, and late-minus-early was
+0.00030 with p=0.493 across 23 pages. It also stopped without registration or
held scoring. Do not reopen it with different bin cuts or position smoothing.

`IL018` tested whether exact two-paragraph herbal descriptions share an
additive first-to-second **interior profile**. All 62 pages aligned in ZL/IT/RF,
and both the opening and closing line of every paragraph were removed before
scoring. No ROOT/FORM interior profile transferred: ZL ROOT gain was -.8353 and
FORM gain -.7994, both on 0/5 positive folds; the max-view p was .661753. IT/RF
likewise had 0/5 positive folds for FORM. This does not test an ordinal relation
carried by entry/closure constructions, explicit D/E-to-q or detached-completion
edges, within-line AII/AI order, the line-local coordinate, or directional
root adjacency. Do not retune the IL018 classifier or simply restore its
boundary lines. A distinct future route may test whether paragraph ordinal adds
held information after explicitly conditioning the known full-paragraph
construction grammar; 3+ paragraph sequences remain untested rather than
negative evidence.

`IL019` completed the genuinely grammar-aware follow-up using all 766 lines of
the same 62 exact two-paragraph pages. It froze 21 confirmed channels across
opening, body, closing, and selected line slopes; 62/68 columns varied and both
boundary/body plants passed. Nevertheless BOUNDARY selected with ZL gain
-.3131, accuracy 43/62, only 1/5 positive blocks, and max-family p=.081397.
IT/RF gains were -.6060/-.4540 with 0/5 positive blocks. FULL was also negative
at -.3527, and FULL-minus-BOUNDARY was -.0397. The accuracy value alone is not
a pass because the frozen held-gain, block, family, and alternate-reading gates
failed. Thus neither IL018's interiors nor IL019's complete confirmed grammar
supports a transferable first/second herbal paragraph profile at this
resolution. Do not tune channels, views, readings, folds, gates, boundary
treatment, or add 3+ pages post-result; a future route needs independent
authorial stage evidence or a genuinely different repeated relation.

`IL014` tested the first post-reset external semantic bridge using only frozen
human-authored textual catalogues. After excluding the five AI-list pages, all
three Dioscoridean witnesses had powered order-free ceilings (29/26/28), but
their physical-order scores were only 9/7/9; 62.5% of 131,072 shuffled Voynich
catalogues did at least as well after max-witness correction. No Dioscoridean
entry text or lexeme is licensed. Do not retune names, synonyms, order
direction, uniqueness, thresholds, or witness selection. A different
historical source requires genuinely new human-authored source data.

`IL015` used a new source-wide Biblissima human index for BnF Latin 6823, with
495 flora records and companion descriptors removed before scoring. It was
also powered but nonconfirming: ceiling 18, ordered score 7 (.389), null median
6, and p=.473141 over 131,072 shuffles. No BnF entry text or lexeme is
licensed. The remaining historical-order route must search a genuinely new
human-indexed witness atlas with global source-selection correction and must
exclude all four already scored witnesses.

`IL016` performed that global search over 8,507 human-indexed flora records in
63 untested Biblissima manuscripts. Seventeen witnesses were powered; the best
had ceiling 12 and ordered score 5 (.417), exactly its null median. After
maximum-witness correction, 130,994/131,072 shuffled catalogues did at least as
well (p=.999405). Exact human-name physical order is therefore not a usable
historical-herbal bridge in this atlas. Do not try another witness, reverse
order, source subgroup, synonym set, or relaxed threshold. A future botanical
route needs independent entry semantics rather than another ordering test.

`IL017` tested that independent-entry-semantics escape hatch with a corrected
born-digital human Dioscorides index. Coverage was ample (84 nonexcluded exact
human-name pages; eight variable medicinal axes), but no axis transferred
across held contiguous folio blocks. WOUND_SKIN's aggregate ZL gain of +.536
came from one block only, with rho .220, 1/5 positive blocks, and joint
max-axis p=.302335; IT/RF were also block-inconsistent. Thus neither historical
order nor these ten broad medicinal-use properties ground the uncertain human
botanical names in Voynich prose. Do not tune the axes, roots, names,
stopwords, folds, alpha, signs, thresholds, or source subsets. A future
botanical route requires a genuinely authorial entry/text relation or new
independent evidence, not another derived property of the same name bridge.

`IL020` stopped the proposed D/E-dependent-to-`che`-boundary bridge before any
model or association score. Only `o`, `od`, `ok`, `ol`, and `ot` have at least
five D and five E dependent events on three pages per state in every reading;
all five are `che` JOIN-preferred and none are SPACE-preferred. Even relaxing
support to one event per state adds only `or`, which is also JOIN-preferred.
The target therefore has one class, and the archived D/E audit had already
found no transferable dependent-root axis after formal-context control. This
is a coverage stop, not evidence that the constructions are unrelated. Do not
fit a classifier, alter thresholds, pool alternate readings as replications,
or infer a root class or meaning. The next text-internal step should first make
a score-blind overlap atlas across the other confirmed construction families
and admit a bridge only if both source and target vary on independently held
roots; exact-pair mining and generic class compression remain closed.

`IL021` completed that score-blind atlas over seven exact root-bearing
construction families and all 21 pairings. No cross-construction association,
model, or held score was run. A first unrecorded render incorrectly counted
rare SPACE exceptions of JOIN-preferred `che` roots as a second root class;
this was corrected before any claim. Under the frozen confirmed `che` classes,
the closest `CHE_BOUNDARY × MINIM_REL_I_GRADE` overlap has seven roots: six are
JOIN-preferred, `H` is unclassified, and zero are SPACE-preferred. The next
closest `CHE_BOUNDARY × DE_SELECTOR_STATE` overlap likewise has only one
usable `che` class. Every other pair is below six shared primary roots, lacks
two supported states, reuses the same event family, or contains a root axis
already shown nontransferable. Thus the present confirmed grammar cannot
support a fair literal-root cross-construction bridge. Do not loosen support,
count exceptions as classes, pool alternate readings, or substitute a new
classifier. The next route needs a genuinely new independent relation or a
mechanism-level system-class prediction that does not depend on shared-root
coverage.

`IL022` completed the first such mechanism-level comparison. It projected
cross-Currier orientation log odds onto the best additive D-root plus C-root
potential and tested only the orthogonal bipartite cycle residual under IL006's
exact conditional null. Validation passed every frozen power, integrity,
source-exclusion, determinism, and negative gate. Final ZL nevertheless reached
only +.00457 bit/edge on 72.2% of 36/44 pages with p=.130649, below both frozen
material thresholds; IT/RF were positive at +.00346/+.00234 but are alternate
readings. Do not retune pseudocounts, shrinkage, weights, supports, roots,
pairs, cycles, signs, splits, or thresholds. IL010 remains a transferable
directional pair relation, but is not upgraded to non-additive cycle structure.

`IL023` then ran the single predeclared sequential complement: the additive
D-root plus C-root potential itself, with every target Currier value excluded
from its source model and a two-component correction for following IL022. All
validation gates passed. ZL gave +.00272 bit/edge on 55.4% of 157/197 pages,
with raw p=.00830, adjusted p=.01659, 79.7% page coverage, and 97.7%
identifiable edges. Both Currier directions and IT/RF were positive. However,
the gain missed the frozen +.005 materiality gate, so the scalar account is not
confirmed and the fixed final Timm scalar controls remained untouched. Do not
retune potentials, pseudocounts, weights, components, roots, pairs, signs,
readings, null cells, thresholds, or score those controls after the failed
primary gate. The scalar/cycle decomposition family is now closed; the next
system-class test needs genuinely different evidence or a different
mechanism-level invariant, not another decomposition of the same adjacency
table.

The next admissible candidate is a hierarchical native-boundary system-class
benchmark. Prior typology controls compared natural-language text poured into
Voynich geometry with one Timm family, but did not calibrate real structured
records or formal notation in their own record boundaries. The manuscript must
not be forced into one class: IL009's shared cross-section assembly establishes
a common low-level substrate, not one document function. Human-catalogued prose,
star-entry, label, and radial unit types must be frozen separately before text
features are computed. A new benchmark must distinguish both uniform and mixed
unseen known corpora before inspecting Voynich, then compare one global class
with a shared substrate plus section-specific classes. Ordinary prose,
structured records including recipes, formal notation or identifier arrays,
and multiple independent non-neural generators are the candidate classes.
Recipe-like star entries and identifier-like astronomical labels are possible
outcomes, not input meanings. A result could choose a rational downstream
strategy but could not itself translate any word.

`IL024` passed its entirely external reference stage but failed on the single
preregistered manuscript score. After two honest source-only instrument
failures, an independently frozen expansion produced .874 source-held
development recall; untouched validation reached .898 macro recall, .875
mixed-section accuracy, and +.614 bits/section over one forced class. On nine
feature-free common-locus manuscript panels, section-specific modeling still
beat one forced class by +.418/+.403/+.439 bits/panel in ZL/IT/RF. However,
only .10/.15/.20 of items lay inside any winning reference envelope and panel
concordance was only 1/9. Circular text was the sole concordant in-envelope
panel and matched only the benchmark's limited `SYNTHETIC` class; this cannot
establish a manuscript class or generator. Do not loosen envelopes, promote
nearest-centroid labels, count alternate readings as replications, or rerun
the same atlas. A new system-class pass requires genuinely new native short-
label/compact-record reference families selected before features; otherwise
the broad external class route is closed.

The permitted post-IL024 source-admission audit has now supplied that genuinely
new evidence without images or OCR/HTR: 11 human-edited CoReMA recipe sources,
15 high-correction Menota diplomatic prose sources, and eight Virtual Mappa
human inscription sources passed provenance and native-boundary admission;
DISHAS was rejected because the available records are standardized tables, not
native diagram labels. Exact extraction retained 11/14/8 sources, frozen by
SHA-256 into development and untouched validation before features. `IL025` is
then completed without ever scoring the manuscript. It required both the full
39-feature view and a 25-feature dynamics-only view that excludes explicit
length/subunit-size/shape summaries. Source-held development passed, and held
full-view validation also passed (macro recall .885; minimum class recall .571;
minimum correct-in-envelope .500; mixed gain +.568 bits/section). The mandatory
dynamic view failed because only .250 of held recipe sources were both correct
and inside the frozen envelope versus the .500 gate, although its macro recall
was .797 and mixed gain +.605. Timm sources again classified as prose. `IL025`
is therefore a validation failure with zero Voynich feature scores. Do not
retune features, envelopes, thresholds, distances, classes, source splits, or
drop the dynamic gate. The native compact-reference class route is closed at
this representation; new work needs independent authorial evidence or a
genuinely different mechanism invariant, not another score of the frozen
Voynich panels. No manuscript class or meaning follows.

The chosen next candidate is a genuinely different mechanism invariant:
pairwise-preserving higher-order line assembly. It asks whether literal-root
order contains a transferable order-two increment after line boundaries, root
counts, and every directed adjacent-root count are fixed. The primary score
must be aggregate rather than selected triplets, use source-excluded
Currier/page transfer, and pass both a planted order-two control and a
pairwise-only negative control before held scoring. This is not the archived
higher-order construction-tag motif test and must not reopen exact-pair mining.
A pass would establish only a reusable three-position dependency beyond
IL006/IL010, not a phrase, POS, language, word meaning, plaintext, or
translation.

A score-blind coverage audit says this candidate is large enough for an
aggregate test. ZL/IT/RF contain 25,779/25,431/26,220 within-line triples; the
ZL validation/test buckets contain 7,330/6,088, of which 3,729/3,112 have an
exact `AB` prefix supported at least five times in training. Page-local
pairing freedom is present on every ZL page, and 4,000 loci are common across
all readings. The vocabulary remains sparse (1,649 ZL root types; 18,961
distinct trigrams), so individual-triplet discovery or glossing is prohibited.
The next gate is a frozen power simulation that must recover a planted
order-two effect while remaining null on pairwise-only data before any held
manuscript score.

That frozen IL026 power calibration has now failed without computing any real
manuscript trigram statistic. The pairwise-only synthetic control behaved
correctly (-.00418 bits/triple; p=.77344), but the planted order-two control,
although +.01105 bits/triple, reached only .580 positive pages and p=.09375
versus the frozen .650 and .01 gates. Thus the raw triple count does not yield
demonstrated transferable power under the exact pairwise/shell/opening/length-
preserving instrument at this sparsity. Do not increase plant strength, alter
smoothing/support/null/gates, or score the sealed manuscript buckets. The
pairwise-preserving order-two route is stopped; no phrase, POS, language,
meaning, plaintext, or translation follows.

**Correction:** the follow-up audit exhausted only a 128-row comment-derived
relation subset, not a complete human inventory of manuscript relations. Its
1,099-row parent is explicitly documented as “a useful index, not a truth
table”; absence of a Stolfi comment means unknown, not unattached. Of those 128
indexed relations, 121 already have current, failed, inventory-covered, or
legacy coverage; six of the seven remaining rows are singletons and the sole
multi-label case is one seven-label island on f75r without an independently
aligned panel or known referent. Those indexed rows cannot ground a word, but a
direct whole-manuscript human relation inventory remains open. It must separate
proximity from ownership and cannot preselect panels by attractive Voynich
strings. Even a repeated relation does not name a lexeme without a readable
referent or a discriminating model prediction.

The user further states that no new authorial anchor is available. Under that
constraint text-only evidence still cannot identify lexical semantics: hidden
concept labels can be permuted while leaving every text-only frequency,
dependency, boundary, and held structural result unchanged. Historical
comparanda can constrain document roles and prevent bad alignments, but they do
not choose English names. Do not present an arbitrary latent relabeling as
translation.

That identifiability audit is now quantified. The same 34 parsed root atoms are
the top-34 set in ZL3b, IT2a, and RF1b. They cover 95.2%/94.6%/95.0% of parsed
root-atom occurrences, fully cover 93.7%/92.8%/93.4% of parsed words, and fully
cover 60.0%/56.6%/58.2% of eligible prose lines. This is an optimistic formal-
composition budget, not 34 word translations: semantic compositionality is not
established, while an exact-root-tuple accounting needs 378/464/384 types for
95% word-token coverage. Even under a restrictive one-to-one 34-concept model,
34! English relabelings remain observationally equivalent. An exact all-reading
set cover identifies five loci (f104v.32, f10v.2, f115v.7, f27v.3, f94r.6) whose
stable root sets jointly touch all 34 atoms; this is a high-information target
packet if a genuine authorial plaintext correspondence is ever found, not a
translation or permission to guess those lines.

A core-first consolidation now separates those 34 atoms by confirmed formal
behavior and renders the five root-cover loci as a neutral interlinear. Eleven
atoms are >=90% BARE in every reading; `ai` and `aii` are >=99.8% BARE and
usually standalone, but remain unordered English meanings rather than numbers
or POS labels. `H`, `ok`, `ot`, `k`, `e`, `cth`, and `ckh` each occupy at least
20 confirmed selector-side dependency endpoints in every reading; `ok`, `ol`,
`ot`, `o`, `od`, and `e` each occupy at least 20 dependent-side endpoints.
Every core atom occurs in at least five sections in every reading, so none is a
section-exclusive plant, bath, or star atom. A compact eight-line diagnostic
packet covers all 34 roots, all six confirmed positive dependencies, all 14
formal roles, bare d/s/t carriers, opening/continuation, and the six major
sections with all-reading agreement. Its strict section lower bound is six
lines; a direct exact combined MILP hit 120 seconds without certification, so
eight is not claimed minimal. This refinement improves a future alignment
target but supplies no lexical gloss or plaintext.

An archive-overlap audit leaves one unresolved anchor-budget question: whether
the ordered atoms of an exact root tuple that is completely absent from
training can predict that tuple's external literal-root selectional
neighborhood after confirmed grammar and page inventory are controlled. Prior
formal-rebuild, observed-partnership, narrow primitive-axis role-completion,
and semantic-label tests do not answer this exact question. The next permitted
gate is therefore a **score-blind tuple-holdout coverage and planted-power
audit**. It must keep all occurrences of each exact tuple in one fold, exclude
target pages, retain each atom in other training tuples, and score only
external left/right literal-root selection beyond the shared formal baseline.
No real manuscript score is permitted unless a planted ordered-atom channel is
recovered and a frequency/length-preserving tuple-to-atom permutation remains
null. A later pass would reduce the likely grounding unit toward the core34; a
powered failure would move it toward the 378/464/384 exact-tuple inventories.
Neither outcome can supply English meanings without an external anchor.

The declared score-blind coverage gate for that route has now failed before
any real external-neighbor likelihood or synthetic power calibration. ZL/IT/RF
retained 6,918/6,993/6,708 held neighbor words, 351/361/341 exact tuple types,
and 96/95/96 pages with zero held-tuple leakage; every count and retention gate
passed. However only the same **21 of 34** core atoms had enough other
multi-atom uses for the frozen side-specific and FIRST/LAST support thresholds,
below the declared 28-atom gate. The excluded 13 do occur inside multi-atom
tuples, but too rarely for this all-core generalization claim. Do not lower the
support or atom-coverage gates, drop the ordered endpoint channel, or silently
reinterpret a result on the 21 supported atoms as evidence for core34. The
34-versus-exact-tuple anchor interval therefore remains unresolved, and no
manuscript compositionality score was computed.

The chosen admissible consolidation was a **hybrid anchor-budget calculation**,
not a supported-subset rerun. It froze the 21 atoms admitted by the score-blind
support audit as candidate reusable components, treated the 13 sparse atoms as
atomic entries when standalone, and retained exact root tuples as exceptions
wherever those components could not assemble the word. This does not test
separable meaning and cannot name a unit. A consensus transcription lattice
was not used because the same 13 atoms are sparse in combinations in all
readings; adjudication cannot create missing independent occurrences.

That hybrid budget is now complete. Requiring coverage separately in every
manual reading, the optimized common inventories contain 31 units at 90%, 116
at 95%, 378 at 97.5%, and 838 at 99%. Each solution uses all 21 supported
candidate components plus 10/95/357/817 exact tuple exceptions. The
conservative exact-tuple-only requirements are 174/464/971/1,487 units. At 95%
the realized coverage is ZL/IT/RF .957/.950/.954, and a deterministic 41-line
packet touches all 116 selected units. These are acquisition bounds, not proof
that the 21 components have meanings or that exact tuples are words.

The label-free information-gathering phase has also been consolidated into a
pre-grounding package built from the locked transcriptions, former confirmed
parser, six confirmed role transitions, and active budget artifacts. Its
15,960 rows and 118,011 space-delimited strings are now correctly described as
the **legacy ASCII-cleaner projection**, not a complete literal transcription.
Direct reconstruction of the three human IVTFF sources finds 15,985 reading
rows, 115,470 source-separated groups, and 99,485 explicit source boundaries.
Exactly 173 source groups produce no legacy ASCII fragment, including 25 whole
reading rows; 2,688 single source groups produce multiple fragments, creating
2,714 nonmanual boundaries. Only 112,609 source groups map one-to-one to one
legacy token. A source-bound atlas now preserves every group, all four
separator states, and the exact legacy mapping. The official STA1 level-0
files now remove the remaining extended-character ambiguity without guessing:
all 15,985 native reading rows, 115,470 source groups, and 99,485 separators
align exactly, and the official edition-specific bidirectional rules reverse
every STA row and group back to its native transcription after removal only of
the inline comments that the STA release deliberately omits. The retained
primary paths contain 469,910 STA symbols; all supplied alternatives use 229
codes in 24 families. ZL3b has 817 explicit alternative sites across 809
groups, all preserved in raw markup. STA is a synthetic comparison alphabet,
not proof of physical letters or sounds; the nearest-basic-EVA field is an
explicitly lossy convenience projection.

Inside that legacy projection, the **partial retained-node formal layer** has
636 root types, 2,835 exact root tuples, and 13,559 descriptive prose adjacency
relations. It retains 114,173 of 118,011 legacy ASCII fragments: 3,838
fragments / 5,237 characters are omitted on 2,833 rows. All 15,960
legacy-surface-to-formal alignments have one unique whole-fragment subsequence,
and the selected fragments equal stored formal node surfaces one-for-one. The
residual atlas preserves the omitted legacy fragments and positions as
`UNPARSED_SURFACE`; they receive no inferred role or meaning. Root, role,
tuple, relation, `word_count`, and exact-space statistics are therefore exact
only inside a partial formal analysis of the legacy cleaner projection. They
cannot support exhaustive source-surface absence or universal boundary claims.

The former omission mechanism is now corrected against source groups. Exact
legacy fragment type perfectly predicts retention in the frozen parser, but
1,801/3,838 “omitted tokens” are fragments created inside one source group by
the ASCII cleaner. Only 2,037 are complete source groups. For `y`, 1,732/2,463
legacy residual events are intra-group fragments; 731 are complete
one-fragment source groups and only 625 have exact raw source spelling `y`.
Thus the former 28-type deterministic inventory describes the combined
cleaner/parser pipeline, not a manuscript category.

The direct alternate-reading natural experiments remain largely intact:
310/312 directed fusion events are source-safe, including 186/188 `y`, all 68
`dy`, and all 22 `ky` events. The two excluded events are one ZL f89r2.9
cleaner split of raw `otold[:y]` against IT and RF. The conservative
mapping-preserving `y` subset is therefore 128/130 rather than 130/130. This
still supports qualified boundary mobility in human alternate readings, not a
suffix, authorial space, role, or meaning.

An apparent next route was stopped after restoring separator metadata that the
normalized `surface` had flattened. In raw manual ZL3b IVTFF, `.` is a
confident apparent space, `,` an uncertain small space, and `<->`/`<~>` mark
drawing interruptions. Among 30 parser-free exact-character `y` disagreement
spans, ZL3b isolates `y` in 28; all 28 already have an explicit uncertain or
drawing boundary and zero have two confident spaces. Thus a proposed context
classifier would rediscover transcription confidence, not an independent
grammar. USR001 and USR002 both stopped unscored; all 90 reading-specific
target groups behind USR002 are exact raw one-fragment groups, so that stop is
unchanged. The former one-neighbor candidate lattice reported 2,178
parser-mapping-preserving events, but 1,602 are intra-source cleaner fragments.
Only 576 complete source groups remain in that descriptive class; for `y`, the
correction is 2,117 to 525. These counts remain compatibility inside the same
partial parser, not a repaired parse. Future surface work must start from the
source-group atlas, preserve the four manual separator states, and exclude
cleaner-created fragment boundaries. That mechanical impact audit and its
independent reconstruction are complete. The immediate next route is a new,
versioned source-aware grammar reconstruction over the lossless STA/source-
group layer, using the old retained-node formal snapshot only as a comparison
target. It must not silently recreate the missing parser or assign semantics.

The source atlas contains 12,093 confirmed-prose reading rows and 3,892
diagnostic-nonprose rows. The legacy formal projection contains 12,092 and
3,868 respectively; one RF prose row and 24 diagnostic rows have no ASCII
fragment. Across legacy physical loci, 1,305 have identical surfaces in
all readings, another 466 have identical retained root sequences, 389 agree
only in retained role sequence, 3,044 disagree more deeply, and 176 are absent
from at least one reading. The package has zero English lexical glosses and
imports none of v0.51's archived contextual overlays. The original formal
parser source is missing from the current tree and reachable Git history, so
the snapshot is byte-frozen but the formal derivation is not currently
reproducible. Future hypotheses must use the lossless STA/source-group atlas
plus the explicit legacy residual atlas, and treat the existing formal columns
as a partial projection rather than a complete occurrence inventory. COL001's frozen
formal-node `i`/`os` counts are unchanged: f2r.15 has no legacy residual and no
legacy residual fragment contains literal `i` or `o`. This is not a complete
source-surface absence claim because 173 source groups vanish before that
legacy layer, and it does not constrain what a future parser might assign to
an unparsed group. SME003 remains closed because it failed
target-free calibration before morphology access.

The first source-native grammar scaffold is now complete. Of 5,214 loci shared
by all readings, 3,934 have exactly the same full STA-family sequence without
edit alignment; 3,572 also contain zero bracketed alternatives. The strict
panel contains 95,451 aligned family symbols. Its 19,709 boundary positions
supported by at least two readings yield 23,281 synchronized construction
groups, each retaining the separate ZL/IT/RF member codes and exact separator
profile. A nonimporting implementation reconstructed 3,934 loci, 23,159 broad
union-boundary rows, 26,184 broad groups, all outputs, and mutation controls in
1,895,831 checks. RF is partly derived from ZL, so agreement is a
transcription-confidence coordinate rather than independent evidence. The next
route may learn a source-boundary grammar only from all-three-versus-zero
positions on training folios, then test whether it ranks held two-reading
positions above one-reading positions. No old formal role or root may be used
as a predictor. The score-blind capacity audit passes: the strict panel has
71,356 zero-reading gaps, 19,041 all-three gaps, 814 one-reading gaps, and 668
two-reading gaps. Both target classes occur on 95 physical folios; every
two-reading gap and 812/814 one-reading gaps have a local family-pair training
example outside their folio. This authorizes freezing the held test, not fitting
after looking at its target contrast and not asserting an authorial word boundary.
The frozen first-order ordered-family-pair test has now run once. It predicts
held unanimous boundaries very strongly (mean folio AUC .93280; all 102 folio
contrasts positive), and the aggregate two-reading-minus-one-reading target is
+.43918, 7.71% of the unanimous contrast, on 64/95 positive folios (exact sign
p=.000462). It nevertheless fails the prespecified reading-anchor gate: the ZL
anchor is +.81290 and RF +.14633, but IT is -.05736 on 70 folios. Because RF is
partly derived from ZL, the aggregate can reflect transcription-specific
boundary conventions rather than one transferable manuscript boundary grammar.
The registered result is therefore **nonconfirmation**, not a weakened pass.
Do not retune this first-order support-count test. Retain only the unanimous and
synchronized source boundaries for the next source-native grammar build.

That source-native build now supplies a confirmed productive edge grammar.
Without using any complete family form as a feature, a leave-physical-folio-out
model of only the first one/two families, last one/two families, and length
distinguishes the first from last synchronized group of a multi-group locus.
On 970 held loci where at least one complete endpoint form is unseen outside
the folio, 810/969 non-tied pairs are correctly directed (83.59%), 97/100 folio
contrasts are positive (exact sign p=1.32e-25), and the equal-folio contrast is
+2.76124 versus +.08623 for length alone. All-three-exact-member, confirmed-
prose, Currier A/B, and 117 both-endpoints-unseen controls remain positive; the
last reaches 87.07% on non-tied pairs. A nonimporting validator reconstructs
the complete score table and result in 531,673 checks. This confirms reusable
source-native construction-edge information on novel complete forms. `FIRST`
and `LAST` are structural positions only, not START/STOP words or meanings.
The frozen edge score does **not** extend as a material smooth coordinate
through the locus interior. On 7,728 mirrored internal pairs it is only 51.53%
correct, 62/102 folios are positive (p=.01853), and the equal-folio effect
+.07580 is only 2.74% of the endpoint reference, failing the frozen 5%
magnitude, 55% accuracy, and p<=.01 gates. The small direction remains positive
under several diagnostics but cannot be promoted. The independently validated
interpretation is therefore an opening/core/closing architecture with strong
edge morphology, not a single scalar progression through the core. Do not
retune the internal-gradient target.

A descriptive decomposition of the confirmed combined edge model now provides
the first source-native structural feature atlas: 28 high-support
`OPEN_EDGE_ASSOCIATED`, 31 `CLOSE_EDGE_ASSOCIATED`, and 138 unresolved feature
values under a fixed >=20-occurrence, |log-LR|>=.5, >=95/102-fold rule. The
clearest single initial is STA family `P` (official basic examples EVA `p/f`),
with 265 first versus 18 last endpoints. Terminal STA family `B` is a common
closing association, with 488 first versus 860 last endpoints. Strong opening
pair candidates include `PL/AL/QL/BL/PA/AK/PK`; strong closing pair candidates
include `KB/AF/AG/KU/UA/BB` and terminal `CA`. These values are descriptive
components of a jointly confirmed classifier, not separately confirmed
operators and not words or meanings.

A complete exact-group decomposition now attaches that architecture to whole
STA-family construction forms. Across 21,899 strict prose group occurrences,
2,856 surfaces, and 94 physical folios, 162 forms meet a >=20-occurrence and
>=10-folio support gate. Conservative 94-fold deletion stability identifies
9 first-associated versus 14 last-associated forms and, independently, 18
edge-associated versus 36 core-associated forms. Strong first tendencies
include `QKJBA/BLJBA/AQKA/DAQKBA/DAQKA/QAC`; strong last tendencies include
`UA/ACA/BA/ABA/AG/ACAB`. A clean implementation reconstructs all 2,856 rows
