### f68v3 inverted-T geometry and water-context retraction

The prior f68v3/Rosettes comparison incorrectly made semantic equivalence of
their similar T-O geometry the working premise. The later attempt to identify
the large lower f68v3 sector as WATER/SEA/OCEAN made the inverse error: it
transferred the content of selected historical analogues into the Voynich
diagram. Both semantic transfers are rejected. Similar partition geometry
does not establish equal content.

Human annotations explicitly place `f68v3.17--19` inside the large lower
sector. In every alternate reading, `ol` is its most frequent parsed root at
5/16 atoms and occurs in 5/9--10 written forms, versus 27--32/244--249 atoms in
the rest of the page. This is a descriptive discovery statistic, not three
independent confirmations. It independently converges with the permitted
archived text-only `X+ol` bathing-side versus `X+od` herbal-side switch.

The complete independent source control instead finds no exact labelled key.
BnF fr.1082 is a binary `terre`/`eaue` globe, while the close elaborate BnF
fr.565 inverted-T has no sector legends. Fully labelled medieval T-O controls
use the same half-plus-two-quarters partition for continents but place the
large half above rather than below. Related diagrams also encode elements,
directions, exposed land/water, and geographic divisions. The strongest safe
reading is therefore only `f68v3.17--19 = TEXT PLACED IN THE LARGE LOWER HALF
OF A T-O-LIKE COSMOLOGICAL DIAGRAM`. The content of all three sectors is
unknown. No form acquires WATER, SEA, OCEAN, continent, or element meaning.
The strict
section-C/hand-4/diagnostic-P/
three-line metadata match has no comparator: it retains only the target. A
fixed descriptive stress test therefore uses the same-page ordinary paragraph,
four short section-C/hand-4 f67v2 blocks, and five same-hand/three-line f67r2
blocks, without p-values. That frozen stress gate fails: the target beats every
control on written-form rate in all readings and on root-atom rate in ZL3b/RF,
but IT2a f67v2.9--10 has 4/12 `ol` atoms versus the target's 5/16. The
upper-left sector label `otodol` also contains `ol`. Thus `ol` remains only a
weak water/sea-domain candidate, not an ocean-exclusive literal WATER word.
The historical water-hemisphere schema remains one candidate interpretation,
not evidence about Voynich content. Broad image/object classification and
proximity ownership remain closed.

That transfer audit is now complete. The two unhedged labels described
directly *on* f82v pools contain `ol` in only 1/2 cases. The two contextual
f82r waterfall labels also contain it in 1/2, while three of seven neighboring
figure labels contain it. This supports at most a broad bathing/water-system
register and contradicts an exclusive WATER lexeme. A narrower exact
construction, `ar+ol`, repeats between the f68v3 lower-sector block and the
f82r left-waterfall label. It is the next fixed candidate, but must first be
audited manuscript-wide against its positional alternatives and non-water
contexts; it currently has no English meaning.

That audit finds a rare but nonlexical recurrence. Exact surface `darol`
occurs at `f68v3.17`, `f75v.21`, and `f82r.35` in ZL/IT, but only the latter two
are exact in all readings; RF reads f68v3 as `arol` while preserving strict
`ar+ol`. The core occurs at 27 physical loci across many sections. Independent
validation corrects the contextual description: f75v.21 and f82r.35 are
unattached proximity/group labels, while f68v3 is an enclosed cosmological
sector whose proposed ocean interpretation came from historical analogy. All five
consensus strict-`ar+ol` label loci are NOT_ATTACHED, and three are in
pharmaceutical plant rows. Prior frozen FLOW/topology tests containing this
family also failed. Therefore WATER, POOL, FLOW, NYMPH, LEFT, OUTLET, and INLET
glosses are not licensed. The only residual lead is a post-hoc positional
written-variant contrast in the `ar+ol` family. A mandatory parser control
shows that the written `s` in `sarol` and `d` in `darol...` are **not** the
confirmed separate bare `s/d` line-entry carriers. The attempted transfer of
that known carrier system to plant-row order stopped before target scoring.
Any new test must first identify the actual strict-parser operation
distinguishing these surface variants and must not borrow the bare-carrier
interpretation.

That operation is now identified. `arol`, `sarol`, and `darol...` instantiate
the already established productive `<PAIR>`, `s<PAIR>`, and `d<PAIR>` surface
frames of the local `ar/al` marker paradigm, while the pre-grounding grammar
normalizes all three cited plant-row forms to `ar+ol`. The row-1/row-2/row-3
staircase is real but entirely disclosed discovery evidence. Broader plant-row
examples place `d<PAIR>` and `s<PAIR>` in multiple row levels, rejecting
universal LOWER/MIDDLE glosses, and the human atlas contains no independent
non-plant ordered `ar/al` panel. Retain the forms as surface-frame variants;
no ordinal structural tag is licensed.

An exact-construction refinement in that lower-sector block survives only as
a descriptive concentration, not as an independent semantic bridge. Under the
correct canonical-unit eligibility, f68v3.17--19 contains `e+ol`, `ar+ol`, and
repeated `e+ol`, with zero eligible universal-base `X+od`: +3/-0 in 9/10/9
written forms but only two positive types. The same-page paragraph is not zero:
it contains +2/+1/+2 `che+ol`, and one f67r2 control contains `che+od` in ZL/IT.
The target rate still exceeds each available fixed comparator, but the feature
is a post-hoc subset of the already failed `ol` localization route, the
format-compatible control population is too small for a useful p-value, and
the upper kind-L labels are ineligible as kind-P controls. The earlier
all-controls-zero and unique-short-paragraph claims came from an incorrect
complete-root-sequence scorer and are retracted. Retain only `[THREE ELIGIBLE
UNIVERSAL-BASE BATHING-SIDE SWITCH OCCURRENCES; TWO TYPES]`. This does not
upgrade any form to WATER/SEA, identify a base, or translate a clause. Freeze
UAX unchanged for a genuinely independent authorially owned P region.

A completed six-witness Oresme audit finds no lost labelled three-sector copy
that can supply meanings or a phrase alignment. BnF fr.1082 has a binary
`terre`/`eaue` globe; the close elaborate BnF fr.565 inverted-T has no sector
words; the other witnesses omit the relevant figure or all figures. Oresme
II.31 is long explanatory prose rather than two upper inscriptions plus a
three-line lower block. No sector-content gloss, Voynich lexeme, or Oresme
clause mapping is licensed.

A broader source-family audit adds ELEMENTAL THREE-SECTOR SCHEMA as a live
historical possibility but no translation. Harley MS 334 supplies a close
fifteenth-century *Image du monde* globe tradition without a catalogued
three-sector legend; other Gossuin witnesses explicitly label four concentric
elements or ordinary geographic divisions instead. Tours MS 947 proves that
`terre`, `eaue`, `airs`, and `feus` could be written directly on contemporary
diagrams, but not in a matching three-compartment key. Related layouts encode
different things and upper positions vary. Therefore WATER/EARTH/AIR remains
a candidate schema only; neither Voynich upper label acquires EARTH, AIR,
LAND, PARADISE, EAST, or WEST.

The earlier “evidence-bounded contextual rendering” is retracted as semantic
evidence. Its neutral structural parse survives: two unknown short upper-sector
inscriptions and a three-line paragraph in the large lower sector. The opening
line contains two bathing-register-side field-state constructions (`e+ol`,
`ar+ol`), `e+ol` repeats in the final line, and the middle line contains a
REL_I construction. The diagram subject, every base, every relation, and every
clause remain unknown. This is not plaintext or a manuscript translation.

The fixed LEFT transfer now fails. Among four held homologous pairs, the
left-`ol`/right-no-`ol` state occurs in only 2/4 ZL, 1/4 IT, and 2/4 RF; f77v
and f83r contain `ol` on both sides. There are no right-only reversals, but all
readings miss the frozen 3/4 gate. Do not translate `ol` as LEFT or mine an
alternative suffix/direction. The T-O-like sector layout and rare
`darol`/strict-`ar+ol` recurrence remain, still without an English lexeme.

### Human label panels and the repeated-plant route

The complete Stolfi label panel recurs in prose, but S97 shows no enrichment
over distinct prose-derived controls matched on length, manuscript ecology,
literal spelling, and formal grammar. The independently reconstructed result
is a qualified nonconfirmation: it rejects enrichment over those selected
ordinary-prose controls, not reference-like reuse in general.

S98 likewise does not confirm a complete-form pharmaceutical-fragment to
Herbal-prose bridge. Its 55 frozen P-versus-A/Z pairs give joined Herbal
specificity `+0.046414` (raw `p=.378981`, Holm `p=1`), only 14 target Herbal
hits, Herbal-count delta `-0.113343`, and 11 wins versus 11 losses. Independent
reconstruction is exact. Scope corrections matter: 1997 supplies the manual
label locations, the P/A/Z classes come from Stolfi 1998 through the validated
crosswalk, the deterministic maximum-cardinality matching is not a global
minimum-cost assignment, and seven target types are container/container?.
Close only this frozen complete-form panel, not every pharmaceutical-to-Herbal
or plant-only stem relation.

New human illustration descriptions satisfy the archived reopening condition
for `CROSS_SECTION_CATCHWORDS`: they identify the same depicted plant on a
Herbal page and a pharmaceutical fragment. Four distinct relations also have
an individually resolvable, unhedged pharmaceutical plant label: f48v↔f89v2
fragment 54/f89v2.6, f18v↔f102r2 fragment 212/f102r2.21,
f23r↔f102r2 fragment 213/f102r2.22, and f19r↔f102v1 fragment
240/f102v1.17. S99 freezes these as a four-way assignment: label root sets must
recover their true same-plant Herbal page better than all 23 wrong assignments
in each alternate reading. The one frozen score is provisionally decisively
negative: primary exact-assignment p-values are `.916667/.916667/.666667`, no
label individually tops its true page, and all three mean true-minus-best-wrong
margins are negative. The rare-root safeguard also fails because ZL/IT retain
no rare root in any of the four labels and RF retains one in only one label.
Independent reconstruction exactly confirms every source join, root/page set,
matrix, assignment, gate, and hash. Ownership strength is unequal: two labels
have explicit attachment metadata and two depend on curated row order plus
unhedged proximity. The result rejects literal parsed-root reuse from these
four labels into their whole Herbal-page prose conditional on those mappings;
it does not reject the human same-plant relations, identifiers absent from
running prose, intact multi-root compositions, paragraph locality, paraphrase,
or every possible stem relation. This route uses manual
descriptions and transcription only—no OCR, automated vision, AI plant
identity, proximity-only ownership, or English-name resemblance.

SNPL001 has now rebound the four drawing relations directly to current public
`voynich.nu` catalogue pages and public manual-transcription records rather
than trusting the archived S99 summary. All four same-plant relations and
old-to-current label loci reproduce. The source detail sharpens the ownership
qualification: f102r2.21, f102r2.22, and f102v1.17 name their plant positions
directly, while f89v2.6 is explicitly next to the target fragment but partly
under its neighbor. A score-blind audit of the later complete source-STA
representation finds four distinct label member sequences and at least one
non-universal four- or five-symbol motif for every alternate reading in both
matched non-target Herbal strata (92 A/hand-1 pages and 5 B/hand-5 pages). The
four-way orbit can attain `1/24=.041667`. This reopens exactly one separately
frozen source-native within-group query; the target Herbal prose remains
unopened, and no public plant relation or capacity fact is itself a name,
meaning, plaintext, or translation.

SNPL002 now calibrates that exact query without target access. It searches for
the label's contiguous four- and five-member STA windows inside one complete
source group, weights them against the appropriate non-target Herbal stratum,
and evaluates all 24 label-to-page assignments. This is neither S99's parsed
unordered root-set score nor S100's exact parsed-word composition. Across 104
frozen synthetic worlds it passes 0/64 nulls, 8/8 same-family edge-mutated
global plants, and 0/8 in every one-label, wrong-pairing, one-reading, and
family-only adversarial family. A clean-room 32-worker reconstruction agrees
exactly. One hash-frozen run on f48v/f18v/f23r/f19r is now authorized with the
public relation order fixed; thresholds may not change afterward. Even a pass
can establish only manuscript-internal same-plant reference signal.

The frozen SNPL002 target is now a qualified nonconfirmation. The public true
diagonal ranks 5th of 24 (`p=.208333`) with margin `-.681900`, and all five
predeclared gates fail in every reconstructed result. Two true pairs contain a
four-member diagnostic recurrence: pharmaceutical `koldarod` shares `daro`
with f18v `daror`, and `loralody` shares `lody` with f19r `qolody`. They are not
selective anchors: `chokam` has an even stronger wrong-page `qokam` recurrence,
`koldarod` also scores highly on the wrong f23r page, and the f48v and f23r true
pairs have no four-member hit. Do not lower the motif length after inspection
or translate `daro`, `lody`, `okam`, or `kold`. Close only this complete
source-group four/five-member reuse mechanism; softer transformations require
genuinely new held relations, not retuning these four targets.

A score-blind S100 audit identifies the untested middle ground between S98 and
S99: grammar-normalized exact multi-root word composition and within-word
ordered root embedding. It is computationally distinct, but it reuses the same
four relations, one label is only a singleton root, and four pairs give only
`1/24` minimum exact p. Its self-tested scorer remains **unrun**. A recovered
November-2025 human comparison table now supplies exact drawing coordinates for
nine good Herbal-pharmaceutical copies, one good Herbal-Herbal copy, and two
explicitly unconvincing controls. Source-bound native inspection now adds a
provisional fifth strongly owned relation: the table's `JSP2025_05` pairs f37v
with f102r1 row 3/item 1, and the official Yale image places f102r1's sole
plant-fragment inscription inside that fragment's pale neck with no competing
fragment in the local writing region. Current metadata maps the unique `@Lf`
record to `f102r1.2`. An independent source/image validator passes ten checks.
This is a machine-authored ownership judgment rather than human annotation,
and the label surface remained sealed through publication of the ownership
result. During the later capacity check, an interactive diagnostic accidentally
displayed the fifth label surface and root sequence to the analyst. No f37v
prose, association score, ranking, or best match was opened; later work must not
be described as fully analyst-blind.

The score-blind five-relation capacity audit stops before scorer calibration.
The added relation fixes the exact-assignment floor at `1/120=.008333` and the
five labels have stable formal-root counts 1, 3, 2, 3, and 5, so four are
multi-root. But only 14/15 label-reading cells are covered by the frozen formal
parser: RF1b f102r2.22 is uncovered. Independent reconstruction passes nine
checks with zero target scores. Do not drop RF1b, use the unresolved parser
output, or run the old S100 scorer. A new attempt requires another externally
fixed singularly owned relation yielding a complete five-relation all-reading
panel, or a new provenance-complete parser.

The recovered-table replacement search is also exhausted. Its four remaining
good Herbal↔pharmaceutical relations supply no sixth singularly owned label:
f102r1 row 3/item 2 has no second plant label; f102r2 row 1/items 1 and 2 are
followed by paragraph text rather than individual labels; and the plausible
f89v2 row 3/item 4 inscription is source-described between items 3 and 4.
Direct inspection of the exact official f89v2 canvas confirms open-whitespace
proximity rather than writing inside item 4. Independent reconstruction passes

text, row order, or proximity as singular ownership.

A post-inspection S101 observation that both root-only copied fragments in the
four S99 pairs have `d`, while both leaf-retaining fragments do not, is not a
reading: its exact 2-by-2 probability is `1/6`, and the sole independent strict
root-only label lacks `d`. Never gloss `d` as ROOT or ROOT-ONLY from this.

S102 tested the coverage of the live semantic route. The recovered human table identifies f39r and
f95r2 as the same complete plant drawing, while both loci contain ordinary
Herbal prose in different quires and hands. The target is one external cell in
the complete 20 hand-2 by 6 hand-3 Herbal-B cross-hand grid, giving 120 candidate
pairs with confirmed ZL3b, IT2a, and RF1b prose. Two score-blind audits agree
that parsed root material must be the sole semantic primary and root-free
grammar only a nuisance control. The selected preregistration retains all 120
cells and calibrates each edge with 65,536 complete-root-payload shuffles within
exact hand, section, Currier, role-shell, atom-count, paragraph-state, and
line-carrier cells. Page length and grammar opportunities therefore remain
fixed, while a crossed residual removes generic endpoint similarity. The
target would have to be the unique 1/120 maximum under the minimum
alternate-reading Z, pass distribution and dominant-root deletion gates, and
reproduce independently. The required root-hidden coverage audit stopped the
route before scoring: f39r passes the 70-percent strong-donor gate at
`.750/.738/.787`, but the six-page hand-3 side gives f95r2 only
`.416/.500/.553`. No root identity, overlap, or similarity was opened, so this
is not semantic evidence against the same-drawing pair. Do not weaken the
shuffle cells. An independent chronology audit rules out the already
score-blind 13-by-5 external-nuisance design on this same target: it predates
coverage, but it was explicitly unselected and the binding preregistration
forbade a fallback after any failed gate. Preserve f39r/f95r2 as lexically
untested background. A new source-blind audit of the cached manual descriptions
finds eight deduplicated Herbal-to-Herbal drawing relations. Five form a clean
one-to-one panel over ten distinct confirmed-prose H/Currier-A/hand-1 pages in
every reading: f17v-f96v whole-plant similarity, f44r-f1v whole-plant
similarity, f6r-f51r leaf similarity, f19r-f2v flower similarity, and
f90r1-f3v bulb similarity. No prior exact relation-conditioned textual use was
found. This five-edge panel is the current validation route; its relation types are human
visual classifications, not translated concepts. The frozen S103 design uses
one raw-count root-atom TF-IDF representation. Exact payload shuffles over the
common 95-page H/A/hand-1 universe calibrate grammar and length only; finite
inference comes from all 120 partner assignments of the ten untouched pages.
A pass requires unique rank 1/120 jointly and in every reading, material
margins, split-half stability, distributed pair/root/locus support,
dominant-pair and dominant-root deletion, and independent reconstruction. The
frozen root-hidden coverage gate passes all 30 endpoint/reading rows: the
minimum strong-slot fraction is `.714286` against the `.70` threshold and the
maximum is `.955556`, using the unchanged exact slot key and 95-page universe.
The first coverage invocation produced no artifact and was corrected because
it mistakenly treated a diagnostic label locus on a prose-eligible page as
metadata drift; the corrected loader skips nonprose loci exactly as specified.
After three target-blind audit/correction cycles, 67 anonymous controls and an
independently validated 13-file manifest authorized one target run. It stopped
at the frozen split-stability gate: maximum half-to-half cell-Z difference is
`.126562` against `.10`, and half assignment ranks differ. The pooled result is
also plainly nonconfirming rather than a near miss: the true relation assignment
ranks `15/120` jointly and `18/14/18` in ZL/IT/RF, every primary gate is false,
and mean diagonal Z is negative in all readings. No ablation, grammar rescue,
root commitment, root identity, or favorable-root inspection ran. A standalone
nonimporting reconstruction passes 48/48 checks with zero discrepancies and a
maximum aggregate numerical difference of `5.22e-15`; it also confirms the
header-only stop table, hashes, sentinel, and second-run refusal. S103 is now
`FINAL_NUMERICALLY_UNSTABLE_STOP`. Do not add draws, relax `.10`, mine subsets,
or switch to ordered composition or PPMI on the same five relations. A
route-novelty audit selects acquisition of an entirely new page-disjoint human
repeated-drawing/component panel as the next semantic route; only after that
panel is frozen may one ordered multi-root written-word test be designed. Keep
f39r/f95r2 separate, keep
f50r/f6r outside the homogeneous panel, and
permanently exclude the lexically exposed f65r label. No outcome by itself
supplies a component word, plant name, language, English gloss, or translation.

That acquisition has now been completed and audited. A text-only crawl
recovered 17 exact Herbal-to-Herbal relations, and a separate public workbook
recovered 164 graded human comparisons. The workbook cannot support an owned
object-label test: zero rows have explicit singular label ownership, and its
stronger ownership-free repeated-A page-bag fallback leaves only nine blocks
(eight with a common rater design), below the frozen ten-block gate.

The best new Herbal-only source panel freezes 16 pages, seven independent
human component-relation families, and 11 exact-new edges. A direct
ROOT/LEAF/FLOWER scan is forbidden because the labels provide neither
authorial text ownership nor explicit negatives and would repeat the closed
human botanical-trait-marker family. The permitted omnibus exact ordered
multi-root test stopped before scoring: 28/48 endpoint-reading rows fail the
frozen exact grammar-slot donor gate, with minimum strong coverage `.142857`.
No tuple identity, overlap, similarity, favourable family, or component word
was opened. Do not rescue this panel by pooling hands, weakening slot keys or
thresholds, deleting families, or changing representation.

The first new text-internal parallel-passage route, PP001, is now closed before
scoring. Exact centered `LEFT–VARIABLE–RIGHT` windows supplied 14 anonymous
odd-folio discovery candidates and four raw even-folio transfers, but the
registered pairwise-preserving null had inadequate movable coverage: only
269/4,797 relevant held occurrences (`.056077`) could move inside the exact
joint grammar cells. The numeric `.70` threshold appeared in the later
coverage code rather than the preregistration, whose wording required only
“sufficient” support; it must not be called a preregistered numeric gate. An
independent prescore audit nevertheless confirms the stop and additionally
finds that the null fails to preserve page vocabulary, ordinal word indexes do
not prove physical cross-reading span alignment, the raw held `T=4` had been
exposed before the complete control instrument was frozen, candidate pairs
could share a root hub, and deletion inference was underspecified. No
candidate identity, context, locus, page pair, permutation rank, or lexical
interpretation was opened. Do not rescue PP001 by weakening grammar cells or
dropping page vocabulary or the LEFT–CENTER and CENTER–RIGHT margins required
for a valid future null.

The next route is CR001, a root-free cross-record system test. Each interior
line in a complete paragraph is represented by the 16 already-defined
nonlexical IL019 channels; after opportunity, metadata, paragraph-mean, and
smooth ordinal removal, a skew-symmetric operator learned on one folio parity
must predict the directed transformation from line `t` to `t+1` on the other
parity better than line `t` to `t+2`. Metadata-only capacity is 258 complete
paragraphs, 90 physical folios, and 1,632 middle-line adjacent pairs. This is
distinct from endpoint bridges, paragraph ordinals, root recency, line-column
similarity, within-line transitions, and PP001 substitutions, and it has no
sparse event-reassignment bottleneck. Exact residualization, orientation-null,
plant, negative-control, concentration, and independent-reconstruction gates
must pass before one target score. An independent prescore audit corrected two
defects before target access: fixed fitted-folio scores cannot simply be
sign-flipped because each folio trains the opposite-parity operator, so every
shared-sign null draw must rebuild both operators from signed equal-folio skew
sufficient statistics; and the original `.05 residual SD` plant was undefined
and unattainable if interpreted as a folio-standardized effect. The amendment
must freeze an explicit synthetic generator and attainable folio-scale power
gate, training-only ZCA whitening, norm/rank guards, and prewhitening family
deletions. Cross-operation same-base external-context transfer remains the
first backup; a strict single-unit specification already leaves four
provisional operation pairs with at least ten anonymous bases in every reading
before its still-required nuisance-donor gate. A CR001 pass could establish a
chained record/process architecture; it still cannot assign English words.

CR001 has now finally stopped at its prescore support/nuisance gate,
before any observed adjacent-minus-skip `B_f`, operator, direction, or effect.
Across the frozen panels, the sparsest channel has a nonzero numerator on only
9 physical folios and a nonzero opportunity on `.106481` of lines, below the
registered 20-folio and `.80` gates. Maximum adjacent/skip nuisance imbalance
is `.107231` and maximum held ordinal correlation `.103427`, both slightly
above `.10`; ordinal R-squared and every ZCA rank/floor/condition gate pass.
Do not tune these thresholds or delete the sparse channel after seeing the
coverage. A nonimporting reconstruction rebuilt all 181,440 channel scalars
exactly and all 48 fold diagnostics within `3.33e-16`, reproduced the same four
failed gates, and confirmed that no `B_f`, operator, effect, score, or target
artifact exists. The exact CR001 representation is closed. It still cannot
assign English words.

That cross-operation route has also finally stopped at coverage before
any context similarity or identity was opened. The frozen donor backoff keeps
`.8795/.9015/.8859` of clean IT/RF/ZL targets. `BARE–BOUND_E` and

but `BARE–FREE_R` retains only `8/8/9` and `FREE_L–FREE_R` only `7/7/8`, while

reconstruction matches every edition total, backoff allocation, pair count,
all 96 operation/parity occurrence and folio margins, and the decision; it
also confirms that no identity or transfer-score artifact exists. Do not
rescue the route by dropping the two failed pairs or relaxing donor or
replication gates. The next route is the score-blind capacity audit for the
distinct joined-versus-detached completion-role transfer already ranked
second in the post-PP001 novelty audit. It still cannot assign English words.

That rank-2 transfer route, JD001, also stops score-blind. Rebuilding the
confirmed paradigm with its later `CORE_ISOLATED` nonoverlap rule leaves only
8--18 distinct anonymous bases per role in every realization/parity/reading
panel, below the frozen minimum of 20. The proposed ordinary role-label shuffle
also cannot preserve both page-by-role and base-by-role margins. The required
exact moves are alternating page-by-base cycles, and even a null-independent
optimistic mobility ceiling is below `.70` in required panels in both

The remaining rank-3 backup, line-level joined/detached realization coherence,
also fails before scoring. Its generous multi-event-line upper bounds are only
78/97 for ZL odd/even and 62/64 for IT, below the frozen 100 lines in every
reading/parity; IT-even also has only 29 candidate pages. The planned
permutation of fitted binary residuals is not exact because `Y-p` values with
different propensities and bases are not exchangeable. No coherence statistic
or identity was opened. All three post-PP001 ranked morphosyntactic routes are

nonduplication audit for a new text-internal invariant or a provenance-traceable
human semantic anchor; no target run is currently registered.

That audit finds no admissible semantic anchor in the current local evidence.
No human annotation family combines explicit one-to-one author-visible
ownership, observed readable contrasting values, independent-folio
replication, unique current-locus mapping, and untouched confirmation data.
The strongest color and star-count panels each occupy only one physical folio.
The tempting explicit-array family classifier is also invalid: section, hand,
and topology absorb all but the Herbal-row versus pharmaceutical-row degree of
freedom, and that remaining contrast is perfectly aliased to quire S versus O.
No manuscript strings or identities were scored in either audit. A targeted
live-web search also found no new admissible public source. The only meaningful
un-ingested near-miss is a 74-row human zodiac-figure attribute layer on f70
and f72, but its label ownership is inferred from spacing/clockwise order and
it spans only two physical folios. Other public tables are aggregate-only,
already tested, duplicate the current annotation lineage, inaccessible as
rows, or lack a text-to-referent owner map. Nothing was ingested.

The selected new text-internal route is a root-free line-local exposed hard-
dependency order test. An independent prescore audit corrected the first
design before target access: all **six** position-local positive `D/E -> q`
adjacencies from the primary atlas must be protected inside immutable maximal
blocks, including `BOUND_D -> Q_BOUND_E`; every counted selector/q match must
have at least one whole intervening block; and unrestricted interior shuffling
is invalid because it erases the known horizontal role gradient. The exact
null must freeze role-blind contiguous early/middle/late zones chosen nearest
one-third/two-thirds visible-word mass, fix both endpoint blocks, and permute
complete nonendpoint blocks only inside their original zones. Inference must
sample the joint line-conditional null rather than use nonexact folio sign
flips. The test then asks only whether remaining exposed selector-side block
endings tend to occur before remaining exposed q-side block beginnings beyond
the established coarse position gradient. This is distinct
from the archived q/D audit, which already rejected balanced-link notation:
the new claim ceiling is non-adjacent **order conditional on unequal counts**,
never balance, stack, resource, word meaning, or system class. The original
1,260/1,268/1,339 capacity and its thresholds are void under these corrections.
The corrected score-blind capacity audit is now complete. After exact
zone-conditioned multiset mobility, nonzero forward and polarity-exchanged
attainable variance, fixed endpoints, and the mandatory intervening-block rule,
ZL/IT/RF retain 170/169/209 eligible lines; 122 lines on 35 physical folios
are eligible in all readings. The exactly count-balanced control retains only
68/65/83 lines, with ZL's 68 spread across 32 folios. This misses the original
provisional 200-line floor, which belonged to the invalid unrestricted null,
so capacity is neither declared sufficient nor stopped. A predeclared
synthetic power calibration must show whether the 68-line ZL balanced panel
can perform its nuisance-control job; otherwise LB001 stops. The builder now
guards the canonical `transcription/sources/ZL3b-n.txt` copy rather than an
identical artifact-path duplicate. No observed order, expectation, residual,
effect, significance, identity, or target artifact exists.

An independent nonimporting reconstruction exactly reproduces all finalized
LB001 capacity counts, all six protected edges, zone cuts and tie breaks,
endpoint exclusions, distinct-multiset arrangement counts, forward/reverse
gap-two variance, the 122-line common-reading panel, and every guarded input
hash. It also confirms that no target-score artifact exists. The only live
question is therefore the still-unrun, prespecified synthetic power calibration;
capacity validation itself is complete.

The first threshold-local synthetic specification was independently stopped
before implementation or execution. Its mixture equation was target-blind and
correct in expectation, but the text had not fixed folio-specific residual
baselines, keyed randomization, calibration uncertainty, independent null
reference batches, or a mirrored polarity plant. Its one-folio concentration
amplitude gate was guaranteed by equal-folio weighting, and its so-called
pairwise-only generator was the conditional null itself. The specification
must be amended and re-audited; the manuscript target remains untouched.

The resulting calibration-only runner was likewise held by an independent
static audit before execution. Its synthetic algebra and post-canonicalization
blinding passed trace review, but it imported the capacity builder before
checking its hash, failed to guard the live interlinear/hard-edge sources and
canonical geometry digest, and could silently overwrite a calibration result.
Those are binding provenance defects; no calibration artifact yet exists.

All three provenance defects were repaired and independently re-audited, after
which the exact admitted runner was executed once on synthetic unordered
geometry only. LB001 stops at that calibration gate: balanced-forward,
mirrored-exchanged, and equal-amplitude four-folio concentration calibrations
pass, but the primary-forward validation interval is
`[+.0547257, +.0610914]`, just below the frozen `+.055` lower bound. The miss is
`.0002743`. The artifact records zero material trials, zero false-positive
trials, no target runner, and no observed within-zone score. Do not rerun with
more draws, change the seed, or relax the interval. A nonimporting validator
has now independently rebuilt the 170/68 panels, canonical geometry, keyed
draws, best-of-16 plants, all 24 numeric calibration scalars, all four pass
booleans, and both concentration counts with exact equality. LB001 is finally
closed at calibration.

The first post-LB001 star-entry field route also stops score-blind. A raw
root-position scalar would refit the already exposed first/body/last gradient
and is not a clean new invariant. The only distinct residual—nonadjacent
root-pair orientation after scalar-position and adjacency protection—has only
10 all-reading pairs at 3/3 discovery/held support, 44 held events, 28 held
entries, and one root in 60% of pairs. The larger 2/2 panel cannot support
direction estimation plus deletion-robust transfer. No root or pair identity
or direction was opened.

The resulting independent route comparison confirms that no unused current
local panel can supply a lexical anchor. A genuinely different predeclared
generator remains a structural fallback, but it cannot choose English labels;
the lexical route requires newly acquired, explicitly owned, readable
authorial contrast evidence. A bounded historical search for exact labelled
homologues of the f67r/f68r diagram topologies found none. *Liber Nemroth* and
Basel UB F.III.8 are useful conceptual near-matches but fail the required
slot-by-slot correspondence, so no historical label transfers.

The explicit fourfold sequence on f57v provides one preserved structural lead,
not a translation. The human transcription marks four 17-item periods; the
normalized 14-symbol views omit or reduce special signs and are not themselves
a period inventory. The corresponding ninth position divides the four periods
two versus two as `f/p`, and this binary partition survives ZL/IT/RF. Position
16 is transcribed `I/c/c/c` by ZL/RF but `c/c/c/c` by IT, so it is retained
only as a reading-sensitive observation and excluded from transfer. A
score-blind confirmed-prose audit finds
22 all-reading `f/p` one-glyph word-pair types, but zero matched formal types
recur on both odd discovery and even held folios; `i/c` has zero prose
minimal-pair types in its diagnostic scan. Therefore no fair contextual-transfer or exchanged-glyph
control exists. Preserve `f/p` only as an f57v-internal binary slot relation;
do not call it sound, allograph, code, semantic opposition, or meaning. The
active action is the bounded search for an exact historically labelled f57v
homologue; absent that new evidence, another prose substitution test is closed.

An independent check of the frozen explicit-array inventory supplies no
cross-folio replication. Among 391 human-registered slots in 46 arrays on 13
physical folios, 17 one-glyph whole-slot pairs remain stable in all three
readings. After excluding isolated single-character labels, seven descriptive
pairs remain, but every glyph opposition is confined to one folio; `f/p`
occurs zero times and the multiword sensitivity scan finds zero candidates.
Thus neither f57v's `f/p` relation nor a replacement fixed-glyph relation can
yet be transferred across diagrams. This is a failure of available replicated
capacity, not evidence that diagram states do not exist.

There is nevertheless one exact single-page semantic lead. Reading the four
human-marked periods clockwise from the NW divider gives the natural sector
order North, East, South, West. The stable position-9 pattern `f/f/p/p` then
matches the human-described figure sexes male, male, female, female, while
front/back and two-arm/one-arm traits alternate. Retain only
`SEX_PARTITION_CANDIDATE`: the same pattern is a purely geometric adjacent-half
split, the trait was inspected before the hypothesis was frozen, and no
independent owned binary panel can currently transfer it. It does not license
`f=male`, `p=female`, MAN, WOMAN, gender morphology, polarity, sound, or code.

A new exact historical comparator was also stopped cleanly. Cod. Sang. 756
p.9, from a first-half-fifteenth-century South German/Swiss mixed geomantic,
medical, astronomical, alchemical, and calendrical manuscript, is a quartered
circular inventory of all 16 four-row binary geomantic figures. This supports
the broad document-practice prior and supplies a sharp proposed falsifier, but
not an f57v match: the historical wheel has 16 total sectors, whereas f57v has
four 17-item periods, and f57v supplies only one stable all-reading binary
column rather than sixteen. Any per-row or per-column glyph-to-bit recoding
would fit the answer. No exact medieval homologue combining the four figures,
four periods, and stable binary variation was found, so no geomantic name or
cardinal label transfers.

The obvious f57v-to-zodiac validation is invalid and was stopped before any
new `f/p` target count. Zodiac masculine/feminine status alternates exactly
with sign order and is already identical to the exposed polarity, hot/cold,
and diurnal/nocturnal partition. An order-preserving null on the ten sign
classes, with the duplicated Aries and Taurus panels kept inside their signs,
has only two distinct assignments: observed parity and global reversal. Its
best attainable one-sided probability is therefore `1/2`. The tempting 252
unrestricted five-of-ten partitions destroy circular order and cannot be
repaired by word-shell or grammar matching. Independent validation of the
f57v sex candidate needs an author-owned binary panel where sex varies
independently of position and those astrological axes.

A separate source-local semantic complement is real but does not transfer.
Human diplomatic comments explicitly connect the four `f67v2.E1` radial
titles to alternating sun/moon path endpoints. In every reading the two
moon-side titles contain normalized root `ol` and the two sun-side titles do
not; the two sun-side titles contain `H/Hd` and the moon-side titles do not.
This perfect 2-versus-2 result occupies one physical folio. The already
consumed, permitted solar/lunar experiment supplies contradictory external
directions: `ROOT=ol` selected SUN or failed its direct gate, while `H` selected
MOON or failed across independent radial, same-page-circle, and Currier-B
panels. Therefore retain only the local complement, never `ol=MOON` or
`H=SUN`; reopening needs a new explicitly title-owned sun/moon panel.

The terminal-`y` four-way observation is also source-audited and strictly
local. The final visible word ends in `y` with clockwise patterns `0101` in
both f57v four-way groups and `1010` in the independently positioned f67v2 E2
group, stable across alternate readings despite substantial word-level
disagreement. The exact same component fails on f68v2 as `1110`; the weaker
Rosettes four-road-point control is adjacent `0011`. Thus terminal `y` may
participate in an alternating state in some radial constructions, but it is
not a universal compass, direction, or phase marker. The post-hoc inner
pre-star f68v2 fragment must not replace the failed final-word rule.

A new cross-ring join gives that f57v alternation a specific local explanation.
R2's only stable all-reading nonconstant column is position 9 with sector states
`f,f,p,p` in North, East, South, West order. Its cyclic state-change vector on
the visible NE, SE, SW, and NW dividers is exactly `0,1,0,1`. Both previously
fixed inner four-item groups have terminal-`y` state `0,1,0,1` on those same
coordinates. D1 is the stronger relation because its radial titles explicitly
lie on the dividers between consecutive figures; N1 remains supporting but
ownership-ambiguous. Retain **LOCAL BINARY SECTOR STATE / BOUNDARY TRANSITION
CANDIDATE**: terminal `y` may mark a change between the adjacent unnamed f57v
sector states. The visible male,male,female,female figures make sex the most
economical page-local interpretation of `f,f,p,p`, but an adjacent-half state
is equivalent and the join was noticed post hoc on one folio. The current Yale
witness visibly contains the diagonal lines, but the human source warns they
may be modern additions; the independently repeated R2 periods retain the same
boundaries while medieval authorship of the drawn lines remains uncertain. Do not translate
`f`, `p`, or `y`; confirmation requires a new author-owned panel containing
both a four-sector state and four intervening boundary records. The closest
current transfer comparator already rejects a universal CHANGE function:
f67v2's four diagonal endpoints alternate Sun/Moon, so all four intervening
cardinal paths separate unlike states, but their terminal-`y` vector is only
`0,1,0,1`. Assigning each cardinal to one arbitrarily selected adjacent
diagonal can manufacture a phased state match and is forbidden.

A newly recovered first-generation Lullian witness is the closest historical
capacity comparator yet found. Ramon Llull's *Art demonstrativa*, Marciana
Lat. VI, 200 [2757], f.4r, contains four element-owned 4-by-4 tables: exactly
four owners with sixteen compartments each. Because each f57v period begins
with common `o`, a page-level parse `common opener + sixteen payload positions`
is historically plausible. It is not a copied cell key: at fixed coordinates
Llull's tables have eight four-way and eight binary-pair columns, while f57v
has only one stable nonconstant all-reading column. The square matrices,
explicit readable owner titles, value alphabet, and cell topology also differ.
The literal seventeen-nodes/sixteen-intervals reading has no visible ticks,
spokes, shared endpoints, or endpoint-owned labels and is rejected. Retain
only a provisional **FOUR-OWNER / SIXTEEN-FIELD SYSTEM ANALOGUE**; transfer no
element, degree, medicine, color, root, word, or plaintext.

The predeclared Morgan lineage test has now found substantially earlier and
closer evidence. Prüfening Cod. 12600 (about 1180--1199) places on adjacent
folios a directionally labelled human microcosm and four separately labelled
human element personifications. Its f.29r independently fixes the same axial
map as Morgan--top/right/bottom/left = `IGNIS/AQUA/TERRA/AER`, paired with
East/South/West/North--while f.30r places `AER/IGNIS/TERRA/AQUA` at upper-left,
upper-right, lower-left, lower-right. The latter preserves the same clockwise
cycle `Fire -> Water -> Earth -> Air` and proves that four human owners can be
the four elements in this exact computistical-astronomical manuscript family.
This promotes a provisional **FOUR-ELEMENT MICROCOSM/PERSONIFICATION SYSTEM**
to the best current page-level interpretation of f57v.

The registered phase discriminator has now found a materially closer witness.
Thomas of Cantimpré's *Liber de naturis rerum visibilium*, BSB Clm 2655
f.105r (late thirteenth century), places four linked nude element
personifications on the axes of a circular cosmogram and explicitly labels
both element and qualities. In the source's displayed orientation,
N/E/S/W = `Fire/Earth/Air/Water`, with quality fields
`dry,dry,moist,moist` and `hot,cold,hot,cold`. Without rotating the source,
these exactly match f57v's already-frozen R2 position-9 `f,f,p,p` field and
its back/front/back/front figure-pose field. The previously fixed
NE/SE/SW/NW terminal-`y` pattern `0,1,0,1` also equals the boundary derivative
of the source-owned dry/moist field.

This was initially retained as a source-fixed phase, but later witnesses show
that the complete element placement is not unique. Keep Clm 2655 as a strong
four-element/personification and quality-state witness, not as the sole live
phase. Its exact DRY-SIDE/MOIST-SIDE interpretation is one candidate among
historical mirror phases. Do not export `f`, `p`, or `y` to prose; do not
assign the ownership-ambiguous nearby labels as element names; and do not
claim any English word or plaintext clause. The closest source-named
iconographic relative, the thirteenth-century
Lausanne Cathedral rose, is unusable for phase: its elemental medallions were
rearranged during the 1894--1899 restoration and the official catalogue says
their current placement remains hypothetical. It supports the personification
family but is neither a replication nor a contradiction; do not use its
displayed order to rotate f57v.

The reconstructed *Hortus deliciarum* f.16v human microcosm is a useful
negative phase control. Direct human reading of Straub and Keller's 1899
facsimile fixes its corners as upper-left/upper-right/lower-left/lower-right =
`AER/IGNIS/AQUA/TERRA` around a central human. This confirms only the broad
human-element/function construction family. It is not an axial four-linked-
person topology, its corner phase differs from Prüfening f.30r, and the
original manuscript was destroyed in 1870. Do not rotate or normalize these
corners onto f57v's axes. Clm 2655 therefore remains the sole strict
source-fixed linked-person phase witness in that topology, but it no longer
fixes the unique live element order after the mirror controls below.

A newly inspected older lineage witness replicates the **quality construction
family but not a unique complete phase**. Avranches, Bibliothèque
patrimoniale, MS 226 f.113v (twelfth--thirteenth century) has a circular wheel
whose displayed axes are N/E/S/W = `IGNIS/TERRA/AQUA/AER` and whose intervening
NE/SE/SW/NW lobes are `SICCUS/FRIGIDUS/HUMIDUS/CALIDUS`. Thus its element
moisture field is `dry,dry,moist,moist`, exactly reproducing f57v R2
position-9 `f,f,p,p` without rotation. Its diagonal quality-pair class is
`moisture,temperature,moisture,temperature`, exactly reproducing the frozen
D1 and N1 terminal-`y` vector `0,1,0,1`.

Avranches's exact DRY/COLD/MOIST/HOT assignment is now superseded as the live
phase. British Library Harley MS 3099 f.157 (ca. 1130--1174) preserves a
world-year-human wheel in Isidore of Seville's *De natura rerum*. Direct human
reading fixes its unrotated axes N/E/S/W as `IGNIS/AER/AQUA/TERRA` and its
intervening qualities NE/SE/SW/NW as `CALIDUS/HUMIDUS/FRIGIDUS/SICCUS`, or
Fire/Air/Water/Earth and Hot/Moist/Cold/Dry. It therefore mirrors Avranches
East-to-West while reproducing the same abstract `1100` axis and `0101`
diagonal partitions. Morphology and geometry alone cannot choose the mirror.

Harley supplies a new tie-breaker not used to find it. The ordinary Latin noun
genders of `ignis`, `aer`, `aqua`, and `terra` are masculine, masculine,
feminine, and feminine, exactly matching f57v's independently recorded
male,male,female,female figures at N/E/S/W. Avranches instead puts feminine
Earth at the male East figure and masculine Air at the female West figure.
Personification sex need not universally follow grammatical gender, and
`aer` has exceptional feminine use, so this is a strong provisional selector
rather than proof.

Retain **PROVISIONAL HARLEY HUMORAL PHASE AS THE BEST INTEGRATED f57v
READING**. Its current page-role translation is:

```text
N = FIRE  / SUMMER / YELLOW BILE, CHOLERIC
E = AIR   / SPRING / BLOOD, SANGUINE
S = WATER / WINTER / PHLEGM, PHLEGMATIC
W = EARTH / AUTUMN / BLACK BILE, MELANCHOLIC

NE = HOT quality position
SE = MOIST quality position
SW = COLD quality position
NW = DRY quality position
```

This single phase explains every frozen binary observation without rotation.
R2 `f,f,p,p` matches both hot/hot/cold/cold and the element noun-gender split,
so `f/p` cannot yet distinguish those functions. Moving clockwise from each
element to the next quality gives Hot/Moist/Cold/Dry; the traditional
active/passive quality classes are `0,1,0,1`, matching both figure view and
D1/N1 terminal `y`. N1 exact `ot` selects Hot and Dry, the two qualities
adjoining Fire.

This exact historical phase now has an independent close replication.
Walters Art Museum MS W.73 f.7v (late twelfth-century England) is explicitly
catalogued as a microcosmic-macrocosmic harmony wheel after Isidore, with
`World / Man / Year` at center and elements, seasons, bodily humors, and
qualities in its arcs. Direct human inspection fixes its displayed axes as
Fire/Air/Water/Earth and its NE/SE/SW/NW qualities as
Hot/Moist/Cold/Dry--exactly Harley's phase, without rotation or reflection.
The following Walters f.8r is a useful topology control: it preserves the same
conceptual system and Fire-to-Air-to-Water-to-Earth cycle but places four
element entries on one half and four humor entries on the other, so it is not
miscounted as another axial replication. Retain **PROVISIONAL HARLEY PHASE
INDEPENDENTLY REPLICATED BY WALTERS W.73**. The f57 diagram-role reading is
materially strengthened, while its Voynich strings remain unglossed.

The four f57v N1 surfaces thus retain a perfect all-reading two-bit table:
NE/SE/SW/NW HOT/MOIST/COLD/DRY have exact `starts-ot,terminal-y` states
`10,01,00,11`. `F57QC001` still prevents promoting it to a decipherment: none
of four complete four-slot EAS001 control groups has the exact `ot`/`y` table,
but 12/344 permissive consecutive four-slot windows do, including one
same-order f71v zodiac window; 69/344 windows admit some post-hoc short-affix
two-bit table. Retain **PROVISIONAL LOCAL COMPOSITIONAL QUALITY-CODE
CANDIDATE** and the complete f57v diagram-role translation above, but not
lexical meanings of the Voynich strings. Do not export `f`, `p`, `ot`, `y`, or
any N1 form to zodiac or prose. Independent reconstruction passes all 12
registered checks.

A new cross-page form lead is retained with a strict transcription and
ownership ceiling. The fixed southwest COLD-position label `f57v.8` and the
top-center bathing label `f77v.3` are both read `olkeedal` in ZL3b; the older
Stolfi label inventory prints `olkchdal` at both locations under different
transcriber codes. IT2a instead has `olkchdal` versus `olkeedal`, and RF1b has
`olkchdal` versus `olkee al`, so complete equality does not survive all
alternate readings. Every reading preserves an `ol`+`k` component in both,
but that component is common at 418 all-reading loci on 95 pages and 16 `L`
loci on 14 pages. Official Yale-witness quality control confirms similar
overall forms but cannot resolve the faint f57 `kch`/`kee` cluster or the f77
space. Human annotation of f77v.3 is hedged proximity and cannot choose the
central nymph over the adjacent double-Y tube. Retain only **PROVISIONAL
f57v.8 COLD-POSITION ↔ f77v.3 SIMILAR-FORM CANDIDATE**. Do not translate `ol`,
`k`, `ol+k`, or the complete form as COLD, and do not assign f77v.3 a
temperature, figure, organ, tube, inlet, or outlet meaning. Reopen only with a
third independently and explicitly owned HOT/MOIST/COLD/DRY value frozen
before its string, or new author-visible evidence resolving f77v.3 ownership.

An exhaustive post-hoc neighbour inventory now places that candidate in its
proper reference class. Against all 868 other-page `kind=L` loci present in
all three readings, `f77v.3` ranks first for the f57v.8 COLD-position form by
minimum then mean corresponding-reading surface similarity. It is the only
edition-specific exact match found for any of the four f57 quality-position
forms (ZL only); there is no all-reading exact match. This is useful candidate
prioritization, not a dictionary result. The first neighbours of the HOT,
MOIST, and DRY-position forms, and the remainder of the COLD list, span plant
fragment, star, Moon, zodiac-figure, container, and unannotated label
contexts. The common signal is therefore label/circular-register morphology
before English semantics. Keep `f77v.3` as the first ownership-source
acquisition target, but do not mine further form neighbours as temperature
evidence or transfer any object word to the f57 labels.

A substantially stronger but still post-hoc cross-page construction now links
the fixed f57 two-bit states to author-visible f77r topology. Human annotations
order `f77r.2` through `f77r.7` in the six segments between the seven openings
of the top tube. In every reading their unchanged `starts-ot,terminal-y` states
are COLD, DRY, HOT, HOT, MOIST, COLD. The five internal boundaries therefore
give COLD+DRY, DRY+HOT, HOT+HOT, HOT+MOIST, and MOIST+COLD. Official-witness
human topology QC finds that precisely the four changed-state boundaries emit
and the central HOT+HOT boundary does not; the four changes instantiate the
classical EARTH, FIRE, AIR, and WATER adjacent-quality pairs exactly once.
Only 4/180 fixed-multiset assignments and 8/4096 unrestricted state sequences
have the complete property. F77r is the sole complete hit among 184 stable
consecutive annotated six-label windows; one f68r1 star window passes only a
broader four-edge control because it contains the invalid HOT+COLD pair. A
nonimporting validator passes 28 checks.

Retain **PROVISIONAL POST-HOC f57→f77r FOUR-STATE TRANSITION CONSTRUCTION**.
This is the best current cross-page semantic-structure lead because it explains
emission versus non-emission, but it is not self-confirming. The cached later
visual puff proposal AIR/WATER/FIRE/EARTH agrees with the transition prediction
EARTH/FIRE/AIR/WATER at zero of four positions. Do not gloss any label, `ot`,
terminal `y`, quality, or puff element. Seek a second independently annotated
segmented system and freeze its topology plus test before opening its strings.

The first deliberately broader transfer falsifier fails and narrows what such
a confirmation must look like. F67v1 has 17 human-ordered radial texts forming
the boundaries of 17 cyclic sectors, all containing stars. If the f77r rule
were a universal graphical-output rule under the natural boundary/sector dual,
all adjacent radial states would differ. Only 10/17, 9/17, and 8/17 differ in
ZL/IT/RF; 7/17, 8/17, and 9/17 equal-state sectors still contain stars. A
nonimporting validator passes 19 checks. Retain **FINAL NONCONFIRMATION OF
UNIVERSAL DUAL-TOPOLOGY OUTPUT-IFF-STATE-CHANGE**. This does not erase the
narrow f77r construction because f67v1 has long text on boundaries and output
inside sectors, the reverse placement. Do not accept another generic circular
or segmented page as replication; require short labels inside apparatus
segments with independently visible active/inactive boundaries.

A topology-only replication-capacity audit now finds no such second system in
the strongest six-label candidates. f69r `K1` and f70r1 `X1` each place six
labels between six star arms, but every boundary has an arm, so neither tests
the inactive half of the f77r rule. f78r `X1` has six illustration labels, but
they are distributed among separate pipe, junction, vat, and pond objects
rather than consecutive segments of one apparatus. Independent source/image
reconstruction passes seven checks. Keep f77r post-hoc and unconfirmed; an
all-active wheel or scattered labels are not replications.

A direct residual-form diagnostic now rejects the strongest lexical upgrade of
the f77r bridge. After concatenating spaces and deleting exactly the two
state-defining features—one leading `ot` and one terminal `y`—the observed
same-state mapping between the four f57 exemplars and six f77r labels ranks
only 4th of all 24 assignments in the exact equal-reading score. Three
assignments are higher; the unique best swaps the HOT-position and
MOIST-position exemplars and therefore supplies no alternative semantic key.
All four state-preserving one-label deletions also fail identity as a unique
optimum. Deleting `f77r.2` `olkchs`, the strongest COLD-position resemblance,
drops identity to 18th of 24. An independent implementation reconstructs 480
assignment evaluations in 21 checks. Retain **FINAL POST-HOC NONCONFIRMATION
OF RESIDUAL LEXICAL QUALITY IDENTITY**. The author-visible transition pattern
remains provisional structural evidence only; it has no four-label lexicon.
The next valid target remains a second same-orientation apparatus with short
labels inside segments and independently visible active/inactive boundaries.

The complete current human exact-locus layer has now been audited for that
capacity without opening Voynich strings. Eleven broad water/apparatus label
units have at least five loci. The exposed `f77r/V1` control is the sole unit
whose six comments all place labels between five successive side branches and
whose independent page description reports four emitting openings. None of
the other ten units has both a boundary-plus-one segment array and a mixed
active/inactive output contrast. A bounded live-source review found only the
same imported voynich.nu, Stolfi, Grove/VIB layers, a discussion questioning
label status, and an interpretation-dependent decipherment that is ineligible
as author-visible evidence. Independent validation passes 21 checks. Retain
**STOP ZERO SECOND SAME-ORIENTATION HUMAN-ANNOTATED APPARATUS**. The current
public annotation layer cannot confirm the f77r bridge; reopen only for a new
provenance-clean human source with the missing explicit topology, not another
generic label, pool, tube, circle, path, or proximity claim.

A later exhaustive simple-feature diagnostic recovers an exact local two-bit
table in every manual reading, but a correction makes it semantically
nonidentifying. Terminal `y` selects the SE+NW diagonal in both N1 and D1; N1
`ot` selects the upper NE+NW pair, while D1 `ok` selects the lower SE+SW pair.
Under the fixed Harley/Walters roles these are also MOIST+DRY, HOT+DRY, and
COLD+MOIST. Geometry and quality are therefore perfectly aliased on this one
wheel, and the two registers are not independent semantic observations because
they reuse the same four coordinates. Retain **PROVISIONAL LOCAL TWO-REGISTER
COORDINATE/QUALITY CODE; SEMANTICALLY NONIDENTIFIED**. The bit table and its
independent 25-check reconstruction stand, but no PASSIVE, POSITIVE, NEGATIVE,
HOT, MOIST, COLD, or DRY gloss may be assigned or exported to `y`, `ot`, or
`ok`.

A complete human-topology census supplies no new replication of that strict
alternation. Its strongest independent control is f69v, whose 28 manually
clocked slots alternate long/short exactly but whose terminal-`y` states do
not. The remaining explicit-array panel has ample raw suffix-`y` counts, but a
fair all-reading null preserving word count, final-word length, and the
root-free terminal formal shell leaves only three movable `y` events in three
arrays, all on f72. No parity score was opened. Terminal `y` remains a local
development-folio signal only.

A later three-route source-only acquisition audit found no external escape
from that identifiability problem. No single readable witness reproduces the
full f69 compound topology; no one-page source reproduces all f68 seasonal and
stellar components; and no unused human annotation family supplies explicit
owner plus readable contrast on at least five folios. Broad analogues remain
system priors only. Reopen any of these routes only for the missing exact
component, not another general visual resemblance.

The best current page-level interpretation of f67v2 is now a **provisional
wind/directional cosmography**, possibly combined with solar/lunar states or
paths. This is based on a provenance-clean human/iconographic conjunction: the
page has four opposed groups of three or four connected faces around a central
square/circle, eight cardinal/diagonal paths, four side blocks, and pipe- or
blast-like connections; Verona Biblioteca Capitolare MS 89 fol. 3r has four
three-faced wind medallions around a central cross, and the wider medieval
wind-diagram tradition combines wind heads, cardinal groupings, name fields,
and text-bearing spokes. The match is materially stronger than generic circle
geometry but is not exact: Verona's twelve names are circumferential, while
f67v2 has 22 loci and no documented twelve-slot name key. This assigns no
orientation, wind name, or Voynich root. f69r is an independently documented
second wind-topic candidate and permits only a root-hidden, grammar- and
exposure-controlled cross-folio bridge audit before any shared identity is
opened.

A source follow-up strengthens the historical system-family prior but closes
that immediate bridge. Verona MS 89 fol. 3r belongs to an explicit four-sector
by three-wind tradition: twelve circumferential names and four three-faced
wind medallions; its fol. 3v poem gives principal/right/left members in each
cardinal group. The detailed human source and official-witness quality control
show that f67v2 is not an exact `3,3,3,3` copy: its defensible corner inventory
is `3,4,4,4`, with only a speculation that a fourth Northwest face may be
hidden. At least two different Voynich `4+8=12` text decompositions are
possible, so the count does not select a name register. An initial `3,3,4,4`
direct-QC count was wrong because the faint fourth Northeast face was missed.
The resulting `ot...`/`ok...` count-marker suggestion is fully retracted:
`ot...` occurs on both the three-face Northwest group and the four-face
Northeast group.

The preregisterable f67v2--f69r radial root bridge has zero honest control
capacity. They are the only section-C/hand-4 pages with outward radial text;
the remaining three pages are inward-only. Restricting direction leaves no
controls, pooling directions leaves only ten page pairs and perfect
construction confounding, root shuffles violate confirmed page inventories,
and exact all-reading role-shell matching yields zero target matches. No root
score or identity was opened. Reopen only with additional exchangeable wind
folios or a one-to-one labelled homologue.

The previously missing Verona MS 89 fol. 3r photograph was recovered from a
public reproduction and directly inspected without OCR or image recognition.
It confirms four **cardinal** three-faced medallions and one circumferential
name register around a central cross. This makes the system-family analogy
firmer but rejects it as the needed one-to-one labelled homologue: f67v2 has
corner face groups and several competing text registers with no author-visible
slot correspondence to Verona. The exact-homologue acquisition branch is
therefore stopped; no Verona wind or cardinal label may be transferred to a
Voynich locus.

A broader exact-homologue audit found a stronger but still non-transferable
system-family comparator. Cava dei Tirreni MS 3 fol. 198v explicitly combines
four principal winds, eight collateral wind heads, and a central Sun/Moon pair.
This materially strengthens the provisional interpretation of f67v2 as a
wind/directional cosmography with solar/lunar organization. It does not repair
the slot problem: Cava is `3,3,3,3` with one central pair, whereas f67v2 is
defensibly `3,4,4,4`, contains two Suns and two Moons in diagonal structures,
and has several competing label registers. Vienna ÖNB Cod. 378 fol. 1v was
also recovered from the official IIIF witness and has four cardinal
multi-faced wind medallions plus concentric/theological registers, again
without a one-to-one f67v2 map. Madrid BNE MSS/19 fol. 120r and the Bari
Exultet variants show that the same historical wind skeleton could replace the
centre with sirens or Christ. Family membership therefore cannot determine a
centre meaning or donate names. The bounded homologue search is stopped; no
wind, direction, Sun, Moon, cardinal, or historical name transfers.

The known f69r comparison with Oxford, St John's College MS 17 fol. 40v has
now been audited against the official Digital Bodleian witness and the current
human f69r locus inventory. It is a materially strong **wind-rose/system-family
homologue**: both diagrams emphasize four cardinal directions with paired
lines, green intermediate directions, and nearly the same outer decorative
count (Oxford 46, f69r 45). The historical labels nevertheless do not map.
Oxford has twelve owned wind-name sectors in four groups of three; f69r has 22
radial text loci, 16 outer-ring text sectors, and six central letter-like
labels, with only approximate many-to-one alignment between the radial and
outer registers. Oxford's centre is blank. No rotation selects a unique twelve
of the Voynich loci. Retain a strong page-level wind/directional-cosmography
prior for f69r, but transfer no wind name, direction, Latin/English lexeme,
plaintext, or translation. Reopen only with a readable historical witness
matching an actual 22-, 16-, or 6-slot f69r text register one-to-one.

A new historical source audit now removes the specific sixteen-versus-twelve
count objection. Three thirteenth-century Matthew Paris/St Albans witnesses
place a position-owned vernacular register of sixteen directions over a
separate learned twelve-wind layer. An independent pre-1345 Galvaneus Flamma
witness likewise combines twelve scientific winds with four maritime
diagonals into sixteen owned directions. In conjunction with f69r's strong
wind-rose prior and exactly sixteen X1 sectors, this makes `f69r.X1` a strong
**16-DIRECTION-REGISTER CANDIDATE**. It does not establish orientation or prove
that the Voynich strings are direction names rather than associated
parameters, explanations, or mnemonics. Transfer no cardinal direction,
historical name, root, lexeme, plaintext, or translation.

The source conjunction is stronger than that initial formulation. The detailed
human f69r inventory records exactly twelve coloured internal stripes: eight
blue as four pairs at the cardinal axes and four green singles dividing the
quadrants. These sit inside the separate sixteen-sector X1 text ring. Thus
f69r itself combines a fourfold twelve-part graphical skeleton with a
sixteen-part outer register, matching the two system cardinalities explicitly
combined in the Matthew Paris/St Albans and Galvaneus witnesses. The best
current functional identification is therefore a provisional **12+16
WIND/DIRECTION CROSSWALK CANDIDATE**, with X1 likely direction-associated.
The twelve marks are graphical stripes rather than owned readable names, so
orientation and exact slot correspondence remain unknown. This adds no root,
direction name, plaintext, or translation.

A direct human phase-quality check now sharpens that system-family match while
confirming why it is not a key. In a schematic sixteen-point coordinate,
f69r's four blank cardinal axes, four green diagonal spokes, and eight blue
flanking spokes exactly match Matthew Paris's four-principal/eight-collateral/
four-unused construction after a 45-degree phase change. But **eight of 32**
rotations/reflections give the same class match: offsets 2, 6, 10, and 14 in
either handedness. The colored spokes are also not author-owned to the separate
X1 text records, and FDC001 already rejects literal four-base root compounding.
Retain a stronger **12+16 WIND/DIRECTION SYSTEM-FAMILY MATCH**, but no start,
orientation, direction name, root, lexeme, plaintext, or translation.

The first distinct f69r outer-register test stopped before target scoring. A
fixed-margin null for a four-anchor compositional organization is mobile, but
one of the sixteen X1 sectors has no root shared by ZL3b, IT2a, and RF1b, so
the frozen all-reading primary panel is incomplete. No phase, effect, root
identity, or target statistic was opened. Do not rescue this by choosing one
transcription or a post-hoc consensus rule; the stop does not weaken the
independent wind/compass page prior.

The next genuinely distinct same-page registration test is complete and
independently validated as a nonconfirmation. Stolfi's fixed same-wedge
clockwise `E1 -> X1` map was scored against all 352 complete product rotations.
The observed literal-root Jaccard is `.131702` versus `.119513` for the other
registrations: excess `+.012189`, exact `p=.286932`, and only 9/15 positive
sectors. Every reading misses the frozen `+.05` sensitivity gate, neighboring
radial shifts score higher, and a single-root deletion can reverse the effect.
Thus mapped E1 and X1 records do not show distributed literal parsed-root reuse
at this resolution. This closes only that mechanism. It does not weaken the
independent `f69r.X1` sixteen-direction-register candidate; the registers may
encode different fields or semantic correspondence without shared literal
roots. No direction, wind, root meaning, plaintext, or translation follows.

The new exact-sixteen source evidence also admitted one genuinely different
within-X1 test, now complete as a validated nonconfirmation. FDC001 asked
whether four evenly spaced X1 records act as literal-root bases for the twelve
intermediate records, under both complete 40,320-assignment ordered-domino
nulls and 99,999 unrestricted synchronous permutations. The observed
max-phase score is `.018519`, below the even/odd null means
`.068479/.072064`; effects are `-.049960/-.053546`, robust `p=.820833`, and
14/15 complete gates fail. Only 5/12 interiors support the predicted direction,
readings do not retain one phase, and all four anchor-vocabulary deletions stay
negative. A nonimporting validator reproduces all target values exactly. This
closes literal four-base parsed-root compounding on X1, not the independently
supported 12+16 wind/direction crosswalk prior. Historical registers may use
distinct names or different fields. No orientation, direction, wind, root
meaning, plaintext, or translation follows.

A proposed root-free four-versus-twelve label-complexity rescue is stopped
before scoring. The historical sources contradict its premise: Matthew Paris
MS 16I graphically distinguishes four cardinals, whereas Galvaneus places the
four maritime diagonals in the special outer register; neither supplies a
transferable prediction that cardinal labels are shorter, simpler, uniquely
colored, or morphologically distinct. The same X1 four-phase partition was
also already exposed by FDC001, so changing the response to word/root-atom or
formal complexity would be a post-hoc reparameterization rather than new
evidence. The only source-stable four-versus-twelve fact is a possible
graphically distinct quartet, which predicts no strings. The active f69r
question is therefore whether a one-to-one historical homologue or independent
author-visible feature can fix X1 orientation; absent that, no direction name
can transfer.

That orientation search is now also stopped. The decorated 02:00 wedge is a
reproducible human transcription delimiter, but X1.1 has no radial partner and
the source itself permits a filler that repairs the empty/text alternation in
an odd 45-sector ring. The exact same-wedge X1/E1 mapping already failed
FXR001. No checked medieval witness makes an extra ray a cardinal marker or
matches f69r's sixteen outer and six central text inventories one-to-one. The
six central items may instead be one word fragmented across wedges, but the
known internal resemblance is post-hoc; a drafted diagnostic was invalidated
before producing a result because it optimized different orientations and
nearest corpus tokens across alternative readings and lacked frozen controls.
Treat the six only as `SIX_FRAGMENT_CONSTRUCTION_UNRESOLVED` and the wedge only
as a coordinate origin.

A 1375 Catalan Atlas tide calculator is the strongest newly located cyclic-
instrument alternative: sixteen sectors represent a 24-hour cycle and four
sectors span each six-hour tide phase, with readable orientation symbols. It
does not match f69r's six central labels or its other register counts and
therefore donates no label, time, tide, number, direction, or root. A bounded
system-class comparison may assess whether it changes the page prior; it is
not an exact homologue or translation key.

The four prose lines above f69r also cannot be promoted to four cardinal-wind
records. Historical texts do organize the twelve winds conceptually as four
principal winds with two collaterals each, but the close Oxford MS 17 witness
places that sequence in one continuous eight-to-nine-line paragraph; Verona
makes the triples visual rather than four prose records. Locally only f68v3 is
a fair complete same-ecology four-line control. No root or formal parallelism
score was opened. The line-count match is post-hoc, undercontrolled, and has no
author-visible line-to-quadrant ownership; it does not weaken the page-level
wind prior.

An exact human-topology audit opens a different paired-folio route while
preventing a premature wind assignment. f68v2 has four star-interrupted sector
records, each flanked by two outward records from central-star points. This is
abstractly compatible with the medieval four-principal/eight-collateral wind
system. However, same-folio f68r3 has the same four sparse sector centres and
eight boundaries, with an overt Moon/star setting and `1,2,3,4` ordinary
labelled-star members. The special seven-star/Pleiades and connecting-tail
labels are not ordinary members. Retain the two panels as a provisional
**PAIRED ASTRONOMICAL FOURFOLD RECORD FAMILY** and wind only as a nonunique
alternative. Earlier valid work did not compare these human-defined grouped
objects. The preregistered paired-panel test coupling all eight boundaries and
all four centre/member groups has now run once and independently validated as
a nonconfirmation. Its primary alignment was nominally positive (effect
`+0.042842`, exact p `14/384 = .036458`, all three alternate readings
positive), but only 3--4 of 12 positions contributed above their assignment
means and one of only two overlapping roots carried nearly the entire effect:
deleting it left `+0.003382`, p `.416667`. This is a sparse resemblance, not a
distributed paired-record correspondence. Close literal parsed-root reuse for
this exact mechanism; do not tune roots, positions, weights, readings, or
groups around it. A post-result failure diagnostic identifies the two overlap
types as generic formal roots `H` and `aii`; `H` is the deletion-collapse type
and already occurs in 2,983/5,376 ZL loci (64/84 circular loci). This explains
the nominal alignment as sparse construction/register reuse more plausibly
than quarter vocabulary and supplies no gloss.

A newly acquired historical witness sharpens that candidate. Laon BM MS 422
fol. 6v (about 820--830, Isidore/computus) visibly organizes twelve months as
four consecutive triplets owned by four season figures: each figure occupies
the middle radial position and extends toward the two flanking months. This is
an exact structural homologue for f68v2's human-defined
`flank-centre-flank x4` register and fits the paired astronomical folio better
than the abstract wind count. In addition, f68r3's clockwise `1,2,3,4`
ordinary-star sectors align with f68v2's four centre clocks, and the one-star
origin aligns with f68v2's outer-ring gap. Promote only a provisional
**FOUR-QUARTER SEASON/MONTH CONSTRUCTION CANDIDATE**. Laon does not match the
stellar layers or donate labels. The already failed root-free triplet grammar
must not be rerun, and the new fixed cross-panel literal-root grouping has now
failed its robustness gates. Retain the system-class candidate only at the
diagram level. No YEAR, SEASON, MONTH, month name, ordinal, numeral, root
meaning, plaintext, or translation follows.

### f85r2 and the Laufenberg medical-regimen image program

A provenance-clean human-source audit gives f85r2 a new provisional page-role
reading. The existing human catalogue fixes the four figures as North pointing
with an empty hand, West holding a flower or ear of wheat, South leaning on a
cane and holding a chain, and East holding a flask, around a central Sun. The
four exact paragraph ranges are N `f85r2.2-.6`, E `.7-.11`, S `.12-.17`, and W
`.18-.23`.

Heinrich Laufenberg's 1429 medical-astronomical *Regimen* has a possibly
author-conceived chapter-IV image program for the four parts of the year, four
elements, and four complexions. Direct inspection of official Karlsruhe Cod.
2790 gives flowering young women for Spring and Summer, a mature Autumn figure,
a hunched Winter elder with staff and hanging beads/rosary, then a physician
raising a urine vessel, followed by four temperament figures. A reduced Berlin
Ms. germ. fol. 1191 independently preserves a pointing chapter presenter,
Winter as an old woman with staff and bead loop, then the urine-inspecting
physician and complexion figures.

Counter-clockwise from North, f85r2 therefore gives a specific collapsed
sequence `pointer -> flower/growing season -> cane+chain elder -> flask`,
parallel to `chapter opening -> non-winter seasons/life stages -> Winter/old
age -> physician/complexion transition`. The central Sun strengthens the
annual/seasonal context. The rare staff-plus-bead-chain elder is the strongest
motif; flower/season is moderate; pointer and flask/doctor are weaker and
contestable.

Retain **PROVISIONAL LAUFENBERG REGIMEN IMAGE-PROGRAM HOMOLOGY, NO DIRECT SOURCE
ID**. The evidence-bounded page-role rendering is: center SOLAR/ANNUAL MEDICAL
ORDER; North INTRODUCTION TO THE FOURFOLD YEAR/ELEMENTS/COMPLEXIONS; West
FLOWERING OR GROWING SEASONS/YOUNGER-TO-MATURE LIFE; South WINTER/OLD AGE; East
PHYSICIAN'S URINE EXAMINATION/TRANSITION TO COMPLEXION CLASSIFICATION. These are
paragraph/illustration role candidates, not Voynich word or clause
translations. f85r2 compresses a longer source program, its counter-clockwise
order is selected by the match, the East object is ambiguous, and the surviving
comparison witnesses postdate the Voynich parchment although the work dates to
1429. No exemplar, copying direction, root, lexeme, plaintext, or language
identity follows.

The first fixed cross-page lexical bridge from this new role map is complete
and independently nonconfirming. `F85B001` compared the f85 North+East
introduction/complexion pair with all eight f57 quality-register labels, then
